==================== FILE: E:\Github Unity Projects\VContainer ComponentAutoBindTool\Assets\Third Party\ComponentAutoBindTool\Scripts\Core\Editor\AutoBindBuildInput.cs ====================

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using UnityEditor;
using BindData = ComponentAutoBindTool.Core.ComponentAutoBindTool.BindData;

namespace ComponentAutoBindTool.Editor
{
    // 同一次扫描的输入，同时供校验、序列化提交和代码生成使用。
    internal sealed class AutoBindBuildInput
    {
        public Core.ComponentAutoBindTool Target { get; }
        public Type HostType { get; }
        public string ScriptPath { get; }
        public string CodePath { get; }
        public string OutputPath { get; }
        public IReadOnlyList<BindData> Bindings { get; }

        public AutoBindBuildInput(Core.ComponentAutoBindTool target, AutoBindGlobalSetting setting,
            IReadOnlyList<BindData> bindings)
        {
            Target = target;
            HostType = target.m_targetScript != null ? target.m_targetScript.GetType() : null;
            var script = target.m_targetScript != null ? MonoScript.FromMonoBehaviour(target.m_targetScript) : null;
            ScriptPath = script != null ? AssetDatabase.GetAssetPath(script) : string.Empty;
            CodePath = AutoBindPathUtility.GetInspectorCodePath(setting, ScriptPath);
            string resolvedPath = AutoBindPathUtility.GetResolvedCodePath(setting, ScriptPath);
            OutputPath = HostType == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(resolvedPath) ? string.Empty :
                Path.Combine(resolvedPath, HostType.Name + ".BindComponents.cs").Replace('\\', '/');
            Bindings = bindings;
        }
    }
}


==================== FILE: E:\Github Unity Projects\VContainer ComponentAutoBindTool\Assets\Third Party\ComponentAutoBindTool\Scripts\Core\Editor\AutoBindBuildPipeline.cs ====================

using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Text;
using ComponentAutoBindTool.Core;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;

namespace ComponentAutoBindTool.Editor
{
    public sealed class AutoBindPipelineReport
    {
        public bool Succeeded { get; internal set; }
        public AutoBindScanResult ScanResult { get; internal set; }
        public AutoBindValidationResult ValidationResult { get; } = new AutoBindValidationResult();
        public string GeneratedFilePath { get; internal set; }
        public bool BindingsUpdated { get; internal set; }
        public bool CodeFileUpdated { get; internal set; }
    }

    public static class AutoBindBuildPipeline
    {
        public static AutoBindPipelineReport RescanAndValidate(Core.ComponentAutoBindTool target,
            AutoBindGlobalSetting setting, AutoBindKeyMapSetting keyMap)
        {
            return Execute(target, setting, keyMap, false);
        }

        public static AutoBindPipelineReport GenerateCode(Core.ComponentAutoBindTool target,
            AutoBindGlobalSetting setting, AutoBindKeyMapSetting keyMap)
        {
            return Execute(target, setting, keyMap, true);
        }

        private static AutoBindPipelineReport Execute(Core.ComponentAutoBindTool target,
            AutoBindGlobalSetting setting, AutoBindKeyMapSetting keyMap, bool generate)
        {
            var report = new AutoBindPipelineReport();
            try
            {
                if (EditorApplication.isPlayingOrWillChangePlaymode)
                    report.ValidationResult.AddError("请退出运行模式后再生成绑定。");
                if (target == null) report.ValidationResult.AddError("AutoBind 目标为空。");
                if (setting == null) report.ValidationResult.AddError("AutoBindGlobalSetting 为空。");
                report.ScanResult = AutoBindHierarchyScanner.CollectBindings(target, keyMap);
                foreach (string error in report.ScanResult.Errors)
                    report.ValidationResult.AddError("[扫描] " + error);
                if (target == null || setting == null) return report;

                var input = new AutoBindBuildInput(target, setting, report.ScanResult.BindDatas);
                AutoBindValidator.Validate(input, report.ValidationResult);
                if (!report.ValidationResult.IsValid) return report;
                if (generate)
                {
                    string source = AutoBindCodeGenerator.BuildCode(input);
                    using (var transaction = new AutoBindGenerationTransaction(target, input.OutputPath, source))
                    {
                        transaction.Commit(input, report);
                    }
                    report.GeneratedFilePath = input.OutputPath;
                }
                else
                {
                    Undo.IncrementCurrentGroup();
                    int group = Undo.GetCurrentGroup();
                    Undo.SetCurrentGroupName("重扫 AutoBind 绑定");
                    try
                    {
                        report.BindingsUpdated = AutoBindSerializedBindingUtility.ApplyCandidate(input);
                        if (report.BindingsUpdated && PrefabUtility.IsPartOfPrefabInstance(target))
                            PrefabUtility.RecordPrefabInstancePropertyModifications(target);
                        Undo.CollapseUndoOperations(group);
                    }
                    catch
                    {
                        Undo.RevertAllDownToGroup(group);
                        throw;
                    }
                }
                report.Succeeded = true;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                report.BindingsUpdated = false;
                report.CodeFileUpdated = false;
                report.ValidationResult.AddError("AutoBind 操作失败：" + ex.Message);
            }
            return report;
        }
    }
}


==================== FILE: E:\Github Unity Projects\VContainer ComponentAutoBindTool\Assets\Third Party\ComponentAutoBindTool\Scripts\Core\Editor\AutoBindCodeGenerator.cs ====================

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Reflection;
using System.Text;
using ComponentAutoBindTool.ViewCore;
using UnityEngine;

namespace ComponentAutoBindTool.Editor
{
    internal static class AutoBindCodeGenerator
    {
        internal const string HostInterface = "global::ComponentAutoBindTool.ViewCore.IAutoBindHost";

        // 只构造源码；写入、保存和导入全部由流水线提交。
        public static string BuildCode(AutoBindBuildInput input)
        {
            Type type = input.HostType;
            MethodInfo ensure = FindBaseMethod(type, "EnsureAutoBind", typeof(GameObject));
            MethodInfo getter = FindBaseMethod(type, "GetGeneratedUIView");
            bool generateGetter = getter != null && getter.ReturnType == typeof(object);
            bool publicOverride = ensure != null && ensure.IsPublic;
            string indent = string.IsNullOrEmpty(type.Namespace) ? "" : "    ";
            var code = new StringBuilder(2048);
            void Line(string text = "") => code.Append(text).Append('\n');
            Line("// <auto-generated>");
            Line("// 此文件由 ComponentAutoBindTool 生成，请通过 Inspector 重新生成。");
            Line("// AutoBind host: " + type.FullName);
            Line("// </auto-generated>");
            if (indent.Length != 0)
            {
                Line("namespace " + EscapeQualifiedName(type.Namespace));
                Line("{");
            }
            Line(indent + (type.IsPublic ? "public" : "internal") + " partial class " + EscapeIdentifier(type.Name) +
                 " : " + HostInterface);
            Line(indent + "{");
            string member = indent + "    ";
            string body = member + "    ";
            Line(member + "[global::System.Serializable]");
            Line(member + "public class UIView : global::ComponentAutoBindTool.ViewCore.IUiViewComponent");
            Line(member + "{");
            foreach (var binding in input.Bindings)
            {
                Line(body + "public " + FormatTypeName(binding.BindCom.GetType()) + " " +
                     AutoBindFieldNameUtility.BuildFieldName(binding.Name) + ";");
            }
            Line(member + "}");
            Line();
            Line(member + "private UIView view;");
            Line();
            string modifier = ensure == null ? "public" : Access(ensure, type) + " override";
            Line(member + modifier + " void EnsureAutoBind(global::UnityEngine.GameObject go)");
            Line(member + "{");
            Line(body + "if (view != null) return;");
            Line(body + "if (go == null || !go.TryGetComponent<global::ComponentAutoBindTool.Core.ComponentAutoBindTool>(out var autoBindTool))");
            Line(body + "{");
            Line(body + "    global::UnityEngine.Debug.LogError(\"[" + type.Name + "] AutoBind 根节点或绑定组件不存在。\", go);");
            Line(body + "    return;");
            Line(body + "}");
            Line(body + "var boundView = new UIView");
            Line(body + "{");
            for (int i = 0; i < input.Bindings.Count; i++)
            {
                var binding = input.Bindings[i];
                string name = AutoBindFieldNameUtility.BuildFieldName(binding.Name);
                Line(body + "    " + name + " = autoBindTool.GetBindComponent<" + FormatTypeName(binding.BindCom.GetType()) +
                     ">(" + i + ", nameof(UIView." + name + ")),");
            }
            Line(body + "};");
            foreach (var binding in input.Bindings)
            {
                Line(body + "if (boundView." + AutoBindFieldNameUtility.BuildFieldName(binding.Name) + " == null) return;");
            }
            var cascaded = new HashSet<Component>();
            int cascadeIndex = 0;
            foreach (var binding in input.Bindings)
            {
                if (!ShouldCascadeAutoBind(binding, input.Target) || !cascaded.Add(binding.BindCom)) continue;
                string name = AutoBindFieldNameUtility.BuildFieldName(binding.Name);
                string local = "cascadeHost" + cascadeIndex++;
                Line(body + "if ((object)boundView." + name + " is not " + HostInterface + " " + local + ")");
                Line(body + "{");
                Line(body + "    global::UnityEngine.Debug.LogError(\"[AutoBind] 子宿主 " + name + " 尚未实现绑定接口，请先生成并编译子宿主绑定代码。\", boundView." + name + ");");
                Line(body + "    return;");
                Line(body + "}");
                Line(body + local + ".EnsureAutoBind(boundView." + name + ".gameObject);");
            }
            Line(body + "view = boundView;");
            Line(member + "}");
            if (ensure != null && !publicOverride)
            {
                Line(member + "void " + HostInterface + ".EnsureAutoBind(global::UnityEngine.GameObject go) => EnsureAutoBind(go);");
            }
            if (generateGetter)
            {
                Line();
                Line(member + Access(getter, type) + " override object GetGeneratedUIView()");
                Line(member + "{");
                Line(body + "if (view == null) EnsureAutoBind(gameObject);");
                Line(body + "return view;");
                Line(member + "}");
            }
            Line(indent + "}");
            if (indent.Length != 0) Line("}");
            return code.ToString();
        }

        internal static bool ShouldCascadeAutoBind(Core.ComponentAutoBindTool.BindData binding, Core.ComponentAutoBindTool root)
        {
            if (binding == null || binding.BindCom == null || (root != null && binding.BindCom == root.m_targetScript)) return false;
            if (binding.BindCom is WidgetViewBase) return false;
            if (binding.BindCom is IAutoBindHost) return true;
            return root != null && binding.BindCom.gameObject != root.gameObject &&
                   binding.BindCom.TryGetComponent<Core.ComponentAutoBindTool>(out var nested) &&
                   nested.m_targetScript == binding.BindCom;
        }

        internal static MethodInfo FindBaseMethod(Type target, string name, params Type[] parameters)
        {
            const BindingFlags flags = BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.Static | BindingFlags.Public |
                                       BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.DeclaredOnly;
            for (Type current = target.BaseType; current != null; current = current.BaseType)
            {
                MethodInfo method = current.GetMethod(name, flags, null, parameters, null);
                // 最近声明决定可见性；不能跳过 sealed / new 隐藏去重写祖先。
                if (method != null) return method;
            }
            return null;
        }

        internal static bool CanOverride(MethodInfo method, Type returnType)
        {
            return method != null && method.ReturnType == returnType && !method.IsStatic &&
                !method.ContainsGenericParameters && method.IsVirtual && !method.IsFinal &&
                (method.IsPublic || method.IsFamily || method.IsFamilyOrAssembly);
        }

        private static string Access(MethodInfo method, Type host)
        {
            if (method.IsPublic) return "public";
            return method.IsFamilyOrAssembly && method.DeclaringType.Assembly == host.Assembly
                ? "protected internal" : "protected";
        }

        internal static string EscapeIdentifier(string name) => "@" + name;

        private static string EscapeQualifiedName(string name) => string.Join(".", Array.ConvertAll(name.Split('.'), EscapeIdentifier));

        internal static string FormatTypeName(Type type)
        {
            if (type.IsArray) return FormatTypeName(type.GetElementType()) + "[" + new string(',', type.GetArrayRank() - 1) + "]";
            Type[] arguments = type.IsGenericType ? type.GetGenericArguments() : Type.EmptyTypes;
            var chain = new System.Collections.Generic.Stack<Type>();
            for (Type current = type; current != null; current = current.DeclaringType) chain.Push(current);
            var result = new StringBuilder("global::");
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(type.Namespace)) result.Append(EscapeQualifiedName(type.Namespace)).Append('.');
            int argumentIndex = 0;
            while (chain.Count != 0)
            {
                Type current = chain.Pop();
                string name = current.Name;
                int tick = name.IndexOf('`');
                result.Append(EscapeIdentifier(tick < 0 ? name : name.Substring(0, tick)));
                if (tick >= 0)
                {
                    int count = int.Parse(name.Substring(tick + 1));
                    result.Append('<');
                    for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
                    {
                        if (i != 0) result.Append(", ");
                        result.Append(FormatTypeName(arguments[argumentIndex++]));
                    }
                    result.Append('>');
                }
                if (chain.Count != 0) result.Append('.');
            }
            return result.ToString();
        }
    }
}

using UnityEditor;
using UnityEngine;

namespace ComponentAutoBindTool.Editor
{
    internal static class AutoBindEditorAssetLocator
    {
        public static T LoadSingletonAsset<T>() where T : ScriptableObject
        {
            string[] assetGuids = AssetDatabase.FindAssets($"t:{typeof(T).Name}");
            if (assetGuids.Length == 0)
            {
                Debug.LogError($"不存在 {typeof(T).Name}");
                return null;
            }

            if (assetGuids.Length > 1)
            {
                Debug.LogError($"{typeof(T).Name} 数量大于1");
                return null;
            }

            string assetPath = AssetDatabase.GUIDToAssetPath(assetGuids[0]);
            return AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<T>(assetPath);
        }
    }
}


==================== FILE: E:\Github Unity Projects\VContainer ComponentAutoBindTool\Assets\Third Party\ComponentAutoBindTool\Scripts\Core\Editor\AutoBindFieldNameUtility.cs ====================

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace ComponentAutoBindTool.Editor
{
    internal static class AutoBindFieldNameUtility
    {
        private static readonly HashSet<string> Keywords = new HashSet<string>(
            ("abstract as base bool break byte case catch char checked class const continue decimal default delegate " +
             "do double else enum event explicit extern false finally fixed float for foreach goto if implicit in int " +
             "interface internal is lock long namespace new null object operator out override params private protected " +
             "public readonly ref return sbyte sealed short sizeof stackalloc static string struct switch this throw " +
             "true try typeof uint ulong unchecked unsafe ushort using virtual void volatile while __arglist __makeref __reftype __refvalue").Split(' '), StringComparer.Ordinal);

        public static string BuildFieldName(string bindName)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(bindName))
            {
                return string.Empty;
            }

            string fieldName = bindName.Replace("_", string.Empty);
            if (fieldName.Length == 0)
            {
                return string.Empty;
            }

            char firstChar = fieldName[0];
            if (firstChar >= 'A' && firstChar <= 'Z')
            {
                fieldName = char.ToLowerInvariant(firstChar) + fieldName.Substring(1);
            }

            return fieldName;
        }

        public static bool IsValidIdentifier(string identifier)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(identifier) || Keywords.Contains(identifier))
            {
                return false;
            }

            if (!IsIdentifierStartCharacter(identifier[0]))
            {
                return false;
            }

            for (int i = 1; i < identifier.Length; i++)
            {
                if (!IsIdentifierPartCharacter(identifier[i]))
                {
                    return false;
                }
            }

            return true;
        }

        private static bool IsIdentifierStartCharacter(char value)
        {
            return value == '_' || IsAsciiLetter(value);
        }

        private static bool IsIdentifierPartCharacter(char value)
        {
            return value == '_' || IsAsciiLetter(value) || (value >= '0' && value <= '9');
        }

        private static bool IsAsciiLetter(char value)
        {
            return (value >= 'a' && value <= 'z') || (value >= 'A' && value <= 'Z');
        }
    }
}


==================== FILE: E:\Github Unity Projects\VContainer ComponentAutoBindTool\Assets\Third Party\ComponentAutoBindTool\Scripts\Core\Editor\AutoBindGenerationTransaction.cs ====================

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;

namespace ComponentAutoBindTool.Editor
{
    // 将可捕获的提交失败恢复到操作前；不承担编辑器进程崩溃后的恢复。
    internal sealed class AutoBindGenerationTransaction : IDisposable
    {
        private readonly Core.ComponentAutoBindTool m_Target;
        private readonly string m_Source;
        private readonly string m_ObjectSnapshot;
        private readonly Scene m_Scene;
        private readonly PrefabStage m_Stage;
        private readonly bool m_IsAsset;
        private readonly bool m_WasDirty;
        private readonly List<UnityEngine.Object> m_DirtyObjects = new List<UnityEngine.Object>();
        private readonly FileSnapshot m_OwnerFile;
        private readonly FileSnapshot m_CodeFile;
        private readonly GameObject m_InstanceRoot;
        private readonly PropertyModification[] m_Overrides;
        private readonly List<string> m_NewDirectories = new List<string>();
        private string m_TemporaryFile;
        private bool m_RefreshSuspended;

        internal AutoBindGenerationTransaction(Core.ComponentAutoBindTool target, string outputPath, string source)
        {
            // 完成之前重扫/手工编辑的 Undo 记录，避免保存后才被延迟标脏。
            Undo.FlushUndoRecordObjects();
            m_Target = target;
            m_Source = source;
            m_ObjectSnapshot = EditorJsonUtility.ToJson(target);
            m_Stage = PrefabStageUtility.GetPrefabStage(target.gameObject);
            m_IsAsset = EditorUtility.IsPersistent(target);
            m_Scene = target.gameObject.scene;
            string ownerPath = m_Stage != null ? m_Stage.assetPath :
                m_IsAsset ? AssetDatabase.GetAssetPath(target) : m_Scene.path;
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(ownerPath))
                throw new InvalidOperationException("请先保存当前场景，再生成绑定代码。");
            if (!File.Exists(ownerPath))
                throw new InvalidOperationException("所属场景或 Prefab 文件不存在：" + ownerPath);
            if (m_IsAsset)
            {
                foreach (Transform child in target.transform.root.GetComponentsInChildren<Transform>(true))
                {
                    if (EditorUtility.IsDirty(child.gameObject)) m_DirtyObjects.Add(child.gameObject);
                    foreach (Component component in child.GetComponents<Component>())
                        if (component != null && EditorUtility.IsDirty(component)) m_DirtyObjects.Add(component);
                }
            }
            m_WasDirty = m_IsAsset ? m_DirtyObjects.Count != 0 : m_Scene.isDirty;
            m_OwnerFile = new FileSnapshot(Path.GetFullPath(ownerPath));
            m_CodeFile = new FileSnapshot(outputPath);
            m_InstanceRoot = PrefabUtility.GetOutermostPrefabInstanceRoot(target);
            if (m_InstanceRoot != null) m_Overrides = PrefabUtility.GetPropertyModifications(m_InstanceRoot);
        }

        internal void Commit(AutoBindBuildInput input, AutoBindPipelineReport report)
        {
            bool codeChanged = !m_CodeFile.Exists || File.ReadAllText(m_CodeFile.Path) != m_Source;
            // 在触碰对象前检查文件权限，并完成临时文件写入。
            m_OwnerFile.CheckWritable();
            if (codeChanged) m_CodeFile.CheckWritable();
            AssetDatabase.DisallowAutoRefresh();
            m_RefreshSuspended = true;
            bool objectTouched = false;
            try
            {
                if (codeChanged)
                {
                    EnsureOutputDirectory(System.IO.Path.GetDirectoryName(m_CodeFile.Path));
                    m_TemporaryFile = m_CodeFile.Path + "." + Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N") + ".tmp";
                    File.WriteAllText(m_TemporaryFile, m_Source, new UTF8Encoding(false));
                }
                objectTouched = true;
                // 生成是对象与文件的持久化提交；仅撤销对象会使两份产物失步。
                report.BindingsUpdated = AutoBindSerializedBindingUtility.ApplyCandidate(input, false);
                if (report.BindingsUpdated && m_InstanceRoot != null)
                    PrefabUtility.RecordPrefabInstancePropertyModifications(m_Target);
                if (report.BindingsUpdated || m_WasDirty) SaveOwner();
                if (codeChanged)
                {
                    if (m_CodeFile.Exists) File.Replace(m_TemporaryFile, m_CodeFile.Path, null);
                    else File.Move(m_TemporaryFile, m_CodeFile.Path);
                    m_TemporaryFile = null;
                }
                if (codeChanged)
                    AssetDatabase.ImportAsset(AutoBindPathUtility.ToAssetRelativePath(m_CodeFile.Path));
                report.CodeFileUpdated = codeChanged;
            }
            catch (Exception failure)
            {
                var recoveryErrors = new List<string>();
                void Restore(Action action)
                {
                    try { action(); }
                    catch (Exception ex) { recoveryErrors.Add(ex.Message); }
                }
                if (objectTouched)
                {
                    Restore(() => EditorJsonUtility.FromJsonOverwrite(m_ObjectSnapshot, m_Target));
                    Restore(() =>
                    {
                        if (m_InstanceRoot != null) PrefabUtility.SetPropertyModifications(m_InstanceRoot, m_Overrides);
                        // 保存后失败可能清除了原来的脏标记；保留所有先前未保存的场景编辑。
                        if (!m_IsAsset && m_WasDirty) EditorSceneManager.MarkSceneDirty(m_Scene);
                        else if (m_IsAsset)
                        {
                            foreach (UnityEngine.Object dirty in m_DirtyObjects) EditorUtility.SetDirty(dirty);
                            if (!m_DirtyObjects.Contains(m_Target)) EditorUtility.ClearDirty(m_Target);
                        }
                    });
                }
                Restore(m_OwnerFile.Restore);
                Restore(m_CodeFile.Restore);
                throw new IOException(failure.Message + (recoveryErrors.Count == 0 ? "" :
                    "；恢复时发生异常：" + string.Join("；", recoveryErrors)), failure);
            }
        }

        private void SaveOwner()
        {
            if (m_Stage != null)
            {
                PrefabUtility.SaveAsPrefabAsset(m_Stage.prefabContentsRoot, m_Stage.assetPath, out bool saved);
                if (!saved) throw new IOException("Prefab 保存失败。");
                m_Stage.ClearDirtiness();
            }
            else if (m_IsAsset)
            {
                PrefabUtility.SavePrefabAsset(m_Target.transform.root.gameObject, out bool saved);
                if (!saved) throw new IOException("Prefab 保存失败。");
            }
            else if (!EditorSceneManager.SaveScene(m_Scene))
            {
                throw new IOException("场景保存失败。");
            }
        }

        private void EnsureOutputDirectory(string path)
        {
            if (Directory.Exists(path)) return;
            EnsureOutputDirectory(System.IO.Path.GetDirectoryName(path));
            Directory.CreateDirectory(path);
            m_NewDirectories.Add(path);
        }

        public void Dispose()
        {
            try
            {
                if (m_TemporaryFile != null && File.Exists(m_TemporaryFile)) File.Delete(m_TemporaryFile);
                for (int i = m_NewDirectories.Count - 1; i >= 0; i--)
                    if (Directory.Exists(m_NewDirectories[i]) && !Directory.EnumerateFileSystemEntries(m_NewDirectories[i]).Any())
                        Directory.Delete(m_NewDirectories[i]);
            }
            finally
            {
                if (m_RefreshSuspended) AssetDatabase.AllowAutoRefresh();
            }
        }

        private sealed class FileSnapshot
        {
            public string Path { get; }
            public bool Exists => m_Contents != null;
            private readonly byte[] m_Contents;
            private readonly DateTime m_WriteTime;
            public FileSnapshot(string path)
            {
                Path = path;
                if (File.Exists(path))
                {
                    m_Contents = File.ReadAllBytes(path);
                    m_WriteTime = File.GetLastWriteTimeUtc(path);
                }
            }
            public void CheckWritable()
            {
                if (Exists)
                {
                    using (new FileStream(Path, FileMode.Open, FileAccess.ReadWrite, FileShare.Read)) { }
                }
            }
            public void Restore()
            {
                if (Exists)
                {
                    if (!File.Exists(Path) || !m_Contents.SequenceEqual(File.ReadAllBytes(Path)))
                        File.WriteAllBytes(Path, m_Contents);
                    File.SetLastWriteTimeUtc(Path, m_WriteTime);
                }
                else if (File.Exists(Path)) File.Delete(Path);
            }
        }
    }
}

using UnityEditor;

namespace ComponentAutoBindTool.Editor

    public class AutoBindGlobalSetting : ScriptableObject
    {
        [SerializeField] 
        private string m_CodePath = "Assets/Third Party/ComponentAutoBindTool/Scripts/ViewBindComponents";

            AssetDatabase.SaveAssets();

                }

==================== FILE: E:\Github Unity Projects\VContainer ComponentAutoBindTool\Assets\Third Party\ComponentAutoBindTool\Scripts\Core\Editor\AutoBindHierarchyScanner.cs ====================

using System.Collections.Generic;
using ComponentAutoBindTool.Core;
using ComponentAutoBindTool.ViewCore;
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;

namespace ComponentAutoBindTool.Editor
{
    internal static class AutoBindHierarchyScanner
    {
        public static AutoBindScanResult CollectBindings(Core.ComponentAutoBindTool target, AutoBindKeyMapSetting keyMapSetting)
        {
            var result = new AutoBindScanResult();
            if (target == null)
            {
                result.AddError("AutoBind 根节点为空。");
                return result;
            }

            if (keyMapSetting == null)
            {
                result.AddError("AutoBindKeyMapSetting 为空。");
                return result;
            }

            var childRoots = new List<Transform>();
            CollectChildRoots(target.transform, childRoots);

            for (int i = 0; i < childRoots.Count; i++)
            {
                CollectTransformBindings(childRoots[i], keyMapSetting, result);
            }

            return result;
        }

        private static void CollectChildRoots(Transform transform, List<Transform> childRoots)
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < transform.childCount; i++)
            {
                Transform currentChild = transform.GetChild(i);
                if (currentChild.name.Contains("NonRoot ==>"))
                {
                    continue;
                }

                // TMP 自动生成的 SubMeshUI 子对象，跳过
                if (currentChild.GetComponent<TMP_SubMeshUI>() != null)
                {
                    continue;
                }

                childRoots.Add(currentChild);
                // 父级可以绑定嵌套根节点，但其后代交由嵌套 AutoBind 自行扫描。
                if (currentChild.GetComponent<Core.ComponentAutoBindTool>() != null)
                {
                    continue;
                }

                CollectChildRoots(currentChild, childRoots);
            }
        }

        private static void CollectTransformBindings(Transform target, AutoBindKeyMapSetting keyMapSetting,
            AutoBindScanResult result)
        {
            string[] nameParts = target.name.Split('_');
            if (nameParts.Length <= 1)
            {
                return;
            }

            string fieldSuffix = nameParts[nameParts.Length - 1];
            for (int i = 0; i < nameParts.Length - 1; i++)
            {
                string prefix = nameParts[i];
                if (!keyMapSetting.TryGetComponentTypeName(prefix, out string componentTypeName))
                {
                    result.AddError($"{target.name}的命名中{prefix}不存在对应的组件类型，绑定失败");
                    return;
                }

                Component component = ResolveComponent(target, prefix, componentTypeName, result);
                if (component == null) continue;

                result.AddBindData($"{prefix}_{fieldSuffix}", component);
            }
        }

        private static Component ResolveComponent(Transform target, string prefix, string componentTypeName, AutoBindScanResult result)
        {
            if (prefix == "Widget" || componentTypeName == typeof(WidgetViewBase).FullName || componentTypeName == nameof(WidgetViewBase))
            {
                if (target.TryGetComponent<Core.ComponentAutoBindTool>(out var nestedTool) && nestedTool.m_targetScript != null)
                {
                    var selected = nestedTool.m_targetScript;
                    if (selected.gameObject != target.gameObject)
                    {
                        result.AddError($"{target.name}的嵌套 AutoBind 目标脚本必须挂载在当前节点上。");
                        return null;
                    }
                    if (selected is WidgetViewBase widget) return widget;
                    result.AddError($"{target.name}使用 Widget 前缀，但目标脚本未继承 WidgetViewBase；普通宿主请使用 Sub 前缀。");
                    return null;
                }
                var widgets = target.GetComponents<WidgetViewBase>();
                if (widgets.Length == 1) return widgets[0];
                if (widgets.Length == 0)
                {
                    result.AddError($"{target.name}上不存在继承自WidgetViewBase的组件");
                    return null;
                }

                result.AddError($"{target.name}上存在多个WidgetViewBase组件，请在同节点的AutoBind目标脚本中指定一个");
                return null;
            }

            if (prefix == "Sub" || componentTypeName == typeof(Core.ComponentAutoBindTool).FullName)
            {
                if (!target.TryGetComponent<Core.ComponentAutoBindTool>(out var nestedTool))
                {
                    result.AddError($"{target.name}使用 Sub 前缀，但节点上未挂载嵌套 AutoBind。");
                    return null;
                }
                var selected = nestedTool.m_targetScript;
                if (selected == null)
                {
                    result.AddError($"{target.name}使用 Sub 前缀，但嵌套 AutoBind 未指定目标脚本。");
                    return null;
                }
                if (selected.gameObject != target.gameObject || selected == nestedTool)
                {
                    result.AddError($"{target.name}的嵌套 AutoBind 目标必须是同节点的宿主脚本。");
                    return null;
                }
                return selected;
            }

            Component component = target.GetComponent(componentTypeName);
            if (component == null) result.AddError($"{target.name}上不存在{componentTypeName}的组件");
            return component;
        }

    }
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using ComponentAutoBindTool.Core;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEngine;

namespace ComponentAutoBindTool.Editor
{
    internal static class AutoBindInspectorDrawer
    {
        private const float BindNameWidth = 150f;
        private const float DeleteButtonWidth = 22f;
        private const int MaxVisibleBindRows = 6;
        private static GUIStyle s_MiniHeaderStyle;
        private static GUIStyle s_TagStyle;
        private static GUIStyle s_DeleteButtonStyle;
        private static GUIStyle s_KeyMapBoxStyle;
        private static Vector2 s_BindListScrollPosition;

        public static void DrawActionSection(Action onAutoBindAndValidate, Action onGenerateCode, Action onClearAll)
        {
            EditorGUILayout.BeginHorizontal();
            if (DrawTintButton("重扫校验", GetPrimaryButtonColor(), GUILayout.Height(20)))
            {
                onAutoBindAndValidate?.Invoke();
            }

            if (DrawTintButton("生成代码", GetNeutralButtonColor(), GUILayout.Height(20)))
            {
                onGenerateCode?.Invoke();
            }

            if (DrawTintButton("清空", GetDangerButtonColor(), GUILayout.Height(20), GUILayout.Width(50)))
            {
                onClearAll?.Invoke();
            }
            EditorGUILayout.EndHorizontal();
        }

        public static void DrawSettingsSection(string namespaceValue, string classNameValue, string codePathValue)
        {
            DrawCompactInfoPair("NS", namespaceValue, "类", classNameValue);
            DrawCompactInfoLine("路径", codePathValue);
        }

        public static void DrawKeyMapTip(AutoBindKeyMapSetting keyMapSetting, ref bool isExpanded)
        {
            EditorGUILayout.BeginVertical(EditorStyles.helpBox);
            isExpanded = EditorGUILayout.BeginFoldoutHeaderGroup(isExpanded, "组件映射表");

            if (isExpanded)
            {
                DrawKeyMapColumns(keyMapSetting);
            }

            EditorGUILayout.EndFoldoutHeaderGroup();
            EditorGUILayout.EndVertical();
        }

        public static void DrawBindComponentFoldout(SerializedProperty bindDatas, ref bool isExpanded, Action drawContent)
        {
            int bindCount = bindDatas != null ? bindDatas.arraySize : 0;

            EditorGUILayout.BeginVertical(EditorStyles.helpBox);
            isExpanded = EditorGUILayout.BeginFoldoutHeaderGroup(isExpanded, $"绑定数据 ({bindCount})");

            if (isExpanded)
            {
                if (bindCount == 0)
                {
                    EditorGUILayout.LabelField("当前没有绑定数据。", EditorStyles.miniLabel);
                }
                else

                    drawContent?.Invoke();
                }
            }

            EditorGUILayout.EndFoldoutHeaderGroup();
            EditorGUILayout.EndVertical();
        }

        public static int DrawBindDataList(SerializedProperty bindDatas)
        {
            if (bindDatas == null || bindDatas.arraySize == 0)
            {
                return -1;
            }

            int deleteIndex = -1;
            HashSet<string> duplicateNames = CollectDuplicateNames(bindDatas);

            EditorGUILayout.BeginHorizontal();
            EditorGUILayout.LabelField("#", GetMiniHeaderStyle(), GUILayout.Width(18));
            EditorGUILayout.LabelField("绑定名", GetMiniHeaderStyle(), GUILayout.Width(BindNameWidth));
            EditorGUILayout.LabelField("组件", GetMiniHeaderStyle());
            EditorGUILayout.EndHorizontal();

            float rowHeight = EditorGUIUtility.singleLineHeight + 4f;
            float listHeight = Mathf.Min(bindDatas.arraySize, MaxVisibleBindRows) * rowHeight + 4f;
            s_BindListScrollPosition = EditorGUILayout.BeginScrollView(
                s_BindListScrollPosition,
                GUILayout.MaxHeight(listHeight));

            for (int i = 0; i < bindDatas.arraySize; i++)
            {
                SerializedProperty bindDataElement = bindDatas.GetArrayElementAtIndex(i);
                SerializedProperty nameProperty = bindDataElement.FindPropertyRelative("Name");
                SerializedProperty bindComProperty = bindDataElement.FindPropertyRelative("BindCom");
                bool hasMissingComponent = bindComProperty.objectReferenceValue == null;
                bool hasInvalidName = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(nameProperty.stringValue)
                    || duplicateNames.Contains(nameProperty.stringValue);

                Rect rowRect = EditorGUILayout.GetControlRect(false, rowHeight);
                DrawBindRowBackground(rowRect, i, hasMissingComponent, hasInvalidName);

                Rect indexRect = new Rect(rowRect.x + 2f, rowRect.y + 1f, 16f, EditorGUIUtility.singleLineHeight);
                Rect nameRect = new Rect(indexRect.xMax + 4f, rowRect.y + 1f, BindNameWidth, EditorGUIUtility.singleLineHeight);
                Rect deleteRect = new Rect(rowRect.xMax - DeleteButtonWidth, rowRect.y + 1f, DeleteButtonWidth, EditorGUIUtility.singleLineHeight);
                Rect objectRect = new Rect(nameRect.xMax + 4f, rowRect.y + 1f,
                    deleteRect.x - nameRect.xMax - 8f, EditorGUIUtility.singleLineHeight);

                EditorGUI.LabelField(indexRect, i.ToString(), EditorStyles.miniLabel);
                nameProperty.stringValue = EditorGUI.TextField(nameRect, nameProperty.stringValue);
                bindComProperty.objectReferenceValue =
                    EditorGUI.ObjectField(objectRect, bindComProperty.objectReferenceValue, typeof(Component), true);

                if (GUI.Button(deleteRect, "X", GetDeleteButtonStyle()))
                {
                    deleteIndex = i;
                }
            }

            EditorGUILayout.EndScrollView();
            return deleteIndex;
        }

        private static void DrawCompactInfoPair(string leftLabel, string leftValue, string rightLabel, string rightValue)
        {
            Rect rect = EditorGUILayout.GetControlRect(false, EditorGUIUtility.singleLineHeight);
            float halfWidth = (rect.width - 6f) * 0.5f;
            Rect leftRect = new Rect(rect.x, rect.y, halfWidth, rect.height);
            Rect rightRect = new Rect(rect.x + halfWidth + 6f, rect.y, halfWidth, rect.height);

            DrawCompactInfo(leftRect, leftLabel, leftValue);
            DrawCompactInfo(rightRect, rightLabel, rightValue);
        }

        private static void DrawCompactInfoLine(string label, string value)
        {
            DrawCompactInfo(EditorGUILayout.GetControlRect(false, EditorGUIUtility.singleLineHeight), label, value);
        }

        private static void DrawCompactInfo(Rect rect, string label, string value)
        {
            const float tagWidth = 32f;
            Rect tagRect = new Rect(rect.x, rect.y, tagWidth, rect.height);
            Rect valueRect = new Rect(rect.x + tagWidth, rect.y, rect.width - tagWidth, rect.height);

            DrawRectBadge(tagRect, label, GetInfoTagColor(), GetTagStyle());
            EditorGUI.LabelField(valueRect, new GUIContent(value ?? string.Empty, value ?? string.Empty), EditorStyles.miniLabel);
        }

        private static void DrawKeyMapColumns(AutoBindKeyMapSetting keyMapSetting)
        {
            var groupA = new List<string>();
            var groupB = new List<string>();
            bool addToGroupA = true;

            if (keyMapSetting != null)
            {
                foreach (KeyValuePair<string, string> item in keyMapSetting.GetAllComponentKeyMaps())
                {
                    string formattedItem = FormatKeyMapItem(item);
                    if (addToGroupA)
                    {
                        groupA.Add(formattedItem);
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        groupB.Add(formattedItem);
                    }

                    addToGroupA = !addToGroupA;
                }
            }

            EditorGUILayout.BeginVertical();
            EditorGUILayout.BeginHorizontal();

            EditorGUILayout.BeginVertical();
            GUILayout.Box(string.Join("\n", groupA.ToArray()), GetKeyMapBoxStyle());
            EditorGUILayout.EndVertical();

            EditorGUILayout.BeginVertical();
            GUILayout.Box(string.Join("\n", groupB.ToArray()), GetKeyMapBoxStyle());
            EditorGUILayout.EndVertical();

            EditorGUILayout.EndHorizontal();
            EditorGUILayout.EndVertical();
        }

        private static bool DrawTintButton(string text, Color color, params GUILayoutOption[] options)
        {
            Color oldContentColor = GUI.contentColor;
            GUI.contentColor = Color.Lerp(oldContentColor, color, 0.80f);
            bool clicked = GUILayout.Button(text, EditorStyles.miniButton, options);
            GUI.contentColor = oldContentColor;
            return clicked;
        }

        private static void DrawRectBadge(Rect rect, string text, Color color, GUIStyle style)
        {
            Color oldContentColor = GUI.contentColor;
            GUI.contentColor = Color.Lerp(oldContentColor, color, 0.9f);
            GUI.Label(rect, text, style);
            GUI.contentColor = oldContentColor;
        }

        private static void DrawBindRowBackground(Rect rowRect, int index, bool hasMissingComponent, bool hasInvalidName)
        {
            Rect insetRect = new Rect(rowRect.x, rowRect.y + 1f, rowRect.width, rowRect.height - 2f);
            Color backgroundColor = Color.clear;
            if (hasMissingComponent)
            {
                backgroundColor = GetDangerRowColor();
            }
            else if (hasInvalidName)
            {
                backgroundColor = GetWarningRowColor();
            }

            if (backgroundColor.a > 0.001f)
            {
                EditorGUI.DrawRect(insetRect, backgroundColor);
            }

            if (hasMissingComponent || hasInvalidName)
            {
                EditorGUI.DrawRect(
                    new Rect(insetRect.x, insetRect.y + 1f, 1f, insetRect.height - 2f),
                    hasMissingComponent ? GetDangerButtonColor() : GetWarningAccentColor());
            }

            EditorGUI.DrawRect(
                new Rect(insetRect.x + 4f, insetRect.yMax - 1f, insetRect.width - 8f, 1f),
                index % 2 == 0 ? GetSeparatorColorA() : GetSeparatorColorB());
        }

        private static HashSet<string> CollectDuplicateNames(SerializedProperty bindDatas)
        {
            var duplicateNames = new HashSet<string>();
            var uniqueNames = new HashSet<string>();
            for (int i = 0; i < bindDatas.arraySize; i++)
            {
                SerializedProperty bindDataElement = bindDatas.GetArrayElementAtIndex(i);
                SerializedProperty nameProperty = bindDataElement.FindPropertyRelative("Name");
                string fieldName = nameProperty.stringValue;
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(fieldName))
                {
                    continue;
                }

                if (!uniqueNames.Add(fieldName))
                {
                    duplicateNames.Add(fieldName);
                }
            }

            return duplicateNames;
        }

        private static string FormatKeyMapItem(KeyValuePair<string, string> item)
        {
            string keyColor = ToHtmlColor(EditorGUIUtility.isProSkin
                ? new Color(0.68f, 0.85f, 0.95f)
                : new Color(0.14f, 0.39f, 0.54f));
            string valueColor = ToHtmlColor(EditorGUIUtility.isProSkin
                ? new Color(0.76f, 0.88f, 0.72f)
                : new Color(0.29f, 0.44f, 0.20f));
            string braceColor = ToHtmlColor(EditorGUIUtility.isProSkin
                ? new Color(0.72f, 0.72f, 0.72f)
                : new Color(0.42f, 0.42f, 0.42f));

            return
                $"<color=#{braceColor}>{{</color> <color=#{keyColor}>{item.Key}</color> <color=#{braceColor}>:</color> <color=#{valueColor}>{item.Value}</color> <color=#{braceColor}>}}</color>";
        }

        private static string ToHtmlColor(Color color)
        {
            return ColorUtility.ToHtmlStringRGB(color);
        }

        private static GUIStyle GetMiniHeaderStyle()
        {
            if (s_MiniHeaderStyle == null)
            {
                s_MiniHeaderStyle = new GUIStyle(EditorStyles.boldLabel);
                s_MiniHeaderStyle.fontSize = 11;
                s_MiniHeaderStyle.alignment = TextAnchor.MiddleCenter;
                s_MiniHeaderStyle.normal.textColor = EditorGUIUtility.isProSkin ? new Color(0.93f, 0.96f, 0.98f) : Color.white;
            }

            return s_MiniHeaderStyle;
        }

        private static GUIStyle GetTagStyle()
        {
            if (s_TagStyle == null)

                s_TagStyle = new GUIStyle(EditorStyles.miniBoldLabel);

        }

#if UNITY_EDITOR
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEngine;
using ComponentAutoBindTool.Core;

namespace ComponentAutoBindTool.Editor
{
    public class AutoBindKeyMapInputWizard : ScriptableWizard
    {
        public string key;
        public string typeName;
        
        private string warningMessage;
        
        protected void OnGUI()
        {
            // 绘制 lowerStr 字段（可编辑）
            key = EditorGUILayout.TextField("缩写前缀:", key);
            
            // 绘制 typeName 字段（只读）
            EditorGUILayout.LabelField("类型名称:", typeName);
            
            // 如果有警告信息，显示帮助框
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(warningMessage))
            {
                EditorGUILayout.HelpBox(warningMessage, MessageType.Warning);
            }

            // 如果需要，添加其他 GUI 控件，例如按钮
            if (GUILayout.Button("确定"))
            {
                OnWizardCreate();
            }
        }

        private void OnWizardCreate()
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(key) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(typeName))
            {
                warningMessage = "组件前缀和类型名称不能为空。";
                return;
            }

            AutoBindKeyMapSetting obj = AutoBindEditorAssetLocator.LoadSingletonAsset<AutoBindKeyMapSetting>();
            if (obj != null)
            {
                if (obj.TryGetComponentTypeName(key, out string existingTypeName))
                {
                    // 前缀一样，但是类型不一样
                    warningMessage = $"警告：键 '{key}' 的类型已存在，当前类型为 '{existingTypeName}', 新类型为 '{typeName}'。";
                    return;
                }

                var targetKey = "";
                foreach (var (strKey, value) in obj.GetAllComponentKeyMaps())
                {
                    if (value == typeName)
                    {
                        targetKey = strKey;
                        break;
                    }
                }
                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(targetKey))
                {
                    warningMessage = $"警告：'{typeName}' 的类型已存在，当前类型的 键 为 '{targetKey}'。"; // 显示警告信息
                    return;
                }
                
                warningMessage = null;

                obj.extraComponentKeyMap ??= new GenericDictionary<string, string>();
                obj.extraComponentKeyMap.Add(key, typeName);
                EditorUtility.SetDirty(obj);
                AssetDatabase.SaveAssets();
                // 关闭窗口
                Close();
            }
            else
            {
                Debug.LogError("未能加载指定的 ScriptableObject。");
            }
        }
    }
}

#endif

#if UNITY_EDITOR
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.Scripting.APIUpdating;

namespace ComponentAutoBindTool.Core
{
    [MovedFrom(true, "Third_Party.ComponentAutoBindTool.Scripts.Core", "Assembly-CSharp", "AutoBindKeyMapSetting")]
    public class AutoBindKeyMapSetting : ScriptableObject
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// 默认组件映射
        /// </summary>
        public readonly Dictionary<string, string> DefaultComponentKeyMap = new()
        {
            { "Tf", "Transform" },
            { "OAni", "Animation" },
            { "NAni", "Animator" },

            { "Rtf", "RectTransform" },
            { "Cav", "Canvas" },
            { "CGroup", "CanvasGroup" },
            { "VLGroup", "UnityEngine.UI.VerticalLayoutGroup" },
            { "HLGroup", "UnityEngine.UI.HorizontalLayoutGroup" },
            { "GLGroup", "UnityEngine.UI.GridLayoutGroup" },
            { "TGroup", "UnityEngine.UI.ToggleGroup" },

            { "Btn", "UnityEngine.UI.Button" },
            { "Img", "UnityEngine.UI.Image" },
            { "RImg", "UnityEngine.UI.RawImage" },
            { "Txt", "UnityEngine.UI.Text" },
            { "Inf", "UnityEngine.UI.InputField" },
            { "Sld", "UnityEngine.UI.Slider" },
            { "Mask", "UnityEngine.UI.Mask" },
            { "Mask2D", "UnityEngine.UI.RectMask2D" },
            { "Tog", "UnityEngine.UI.Toggle" },
            { "Sbr", "UnityEngine.UI.Scrollbar" },
            { "SRect", "UnityEngine.UI.ScrollRect" },
            { "Drop", "UnityEngine.UI.Dropdown" },
                
            { "TMTxt", "TMPro.TextMeshProUGUI" },
            { "TMDrop", "TMPro.TMP_Dropdown" },
            { "TMInf", "TMPro.TMP_InputField" },
            { "Widget", "ComponentAutoBindTool.ViewCore.WidgetViewBase" },
            { "Sub", "ComponentAutoBindTool.Core.ComponentAutoBindTool" },
        };
        
        public GenericDictionary<string, string> extraComponentKeyMap;

        public bool TryGetComponentTypeName(string key, out string componentTypeName)
        {
            if (DefaultComponentKeyMap.TryGetValue(key, out componentTypeName))
            {
                return true;
            }

            if (extraComponentKeyMap != null && extraComponentKeyMap.TryGetValue(key, out componentTypeName))
            {
                return true;
            }

            componentTypeName = null;
            return false;
        }

        public IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> GetAllComponentKeyMaps()
        {
            foreach (KeyValuePair<string, string> item in DefaultComponentKeyMap.OrderBy(pair => pair.Key))
            {
                yield return item;
            }

            if (extraComponentKeyMap == null)
            {
                yield break;
            }

            foreach (KeyValuePair<string, string> item in extraComponentKeyMap.Where(pair => !DefaultComponentKeyMap.ContainsKey(pair.Key)).OrderBy(pair => pair.Key))
            {
                yield return item;
            }
        }

        [MenuItem("自动绑定UI组件/创建组件映射文件")]
        private static void CreateAutoBindKeyMapSetting()
        {
            string[] paths = AssetDatabase.FindAssets("t:AutoBindKeyMapSetting");
            if (paths.Length >= 1)
            {
                string path = AssetDatabase.GUIDToAssetPath(paths[0]);
                EditorUtility.DisplayDialog("警告", $"已存在AutoBindKeyMapSetting，路径:{path}", "确认");
                return;
            }

            AutoBindKeyMapSetting setting = CreateInstance<AutoBindKeyMapSetting>();
            AssetDatabase.CreateAsset(setting, "Assets/Third Party/ComponentAutoBindTool/AutoBindKeyMapSetting.asset");
            AssetDatabase.SaveAssets();
            AssetDatabase.Refresh();
        }
    }
}
#endif


==================== FILE: E:\Github Unity Projects\VContainer ComponentAutoBindTool\Assets\Third Party\ComponentAutoBindTool\Scripts\Core\Editor\AutoBindKeyMapSettingEditor.cs ====================

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using ComponentAutoBindTool.Core;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEngine;

#if UNITY_EDITOR
namespace ComponentAutoBindTool.Editor
{
    [CustomEditor(typeof(AutoBindKeyMapSetting))]
    public class AutoBindKeyMapSettingEditor : UnityEditor.Editor
    {
        private SerializedProperty _extraComponentKeyMapProperty;
        private bool _showDefaultKeyMap = true; // 控制折叠状态
        private static HashSet<string> s_ComponentNames;

        private void OnEnable()
        {
            _extraComponentKeyMapProperty = serializedObject.FindProperty("extraComponentKeyMap");
        }

        public override void OnInspectorGUI()
        {
            var autoBindKeyMapSetting = (AutoBindKeyMapSetting)target;
            
            serializedObject.Update();

            // 显示可折叠的字典
            _showDefaultKeyMap = EditorGUILayout.Foldout(_showDefaultKeyMap, 
                "Default Component Key Map", true);
            if (_showDefaultKeyMap)
            {
                EditorGUI.indentLevel++; // 增加缩进
                foreach (var keyValuePair in autoBindKeyMapSetting.DefaultComponentKeyMap)
                {                    
                    EditorGUILayout.LabelField($"组件缩写: {keyValuePair.Key}", 
                        $"组件名称: {keyValuePair.Value}");
                }
                EditorGUI.indentLevel--; // 恢复缩进
            }

            // 显示可编辑字典
            EditorGUILayout.PropertyField(_extraComponentKeyMapProperty, true);

            serializedObject.ApplyModifiedProperties();
            DrawInvalidMappings();
        }

        private void DrawInvalidMappings()
        {
            serializedObject.Update();
            var list = _extraComponentKeyMapProperty.FindPropertyRelative("list");
            if (list == null) return;
            var invalidIndices = new List<int>();
            for (int i = 0; i < list.arraySize; i++)
            {
                var item = list.GetArrayElementAtIndex(i);
                string typeName = item.FindPropertyRelative("Value").stringValue;
                if (!IsComponentTypeName(typeName))
                {
                    invalidIndices.Add(i);
                    string key = item.FindPropertyRelative("Key").stringValue;
                    EditorGUILayout.HelpBox($"映射 {key} 的组件类型无法解析：{typeName}", MessageType.Warning);
                }
            }

            using (new EditorGUI.DisabledScope(invalidIndices.Count == 0 || EditorApplication.isCompiling || EditorApplication.isPlayingOrWillChangePlaymode))
            {
                if (GUILayout.Button("清理失效映射"))
                {
                    RemoveInvalidMappings(serializedObject);
                }
            }
        }

        internal static int RemoveInvalidMappings(SerializedObject settings)
        {
            settings.Update();
            var list = settings.FindProperty("extraComponentKeyMap").FindPropertyRelative("list");
            if (list == null) return 0;
            int count = 0;
            for (int i = list.arraySize - 1; i >= 0; i--)
            {
                var value = list.GetArrayElementAtIndex(i).FindPropertyRelative("Value").stringValue;
                if (IsComponentTypeName(value)) continue;
                list.DeleteArrayElementAtIndex(i);
                count++;
            }
            if (count > 0) settings.ApplyModifiedProperties();
            return count;
        }

        internal static bool IsComponentTypeName(string typeName)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(typeName)) return false;
            if (s_ComponentNames == null)
            {
                s_ComponentNames = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.Ordinal)
                {
                    typeof(Component).Name,
                    typeof(Component).FullName
                };
                foreach (var type in TypeCache.GetTypesDerivedFrom<Component>())
                {
                    s_ComponentNames.Add(type.Name);
                    if (type.FullName != null) s_ComponentNames.Add(type.FullName);
                }
            }
            return s_ComponentNames.Contains(typeName);
        }
    }
}
#endif


==================== FILE: E:\Github Unity Projects\VContainer ComponentAutoBindTool\Assets\Third Party\ComponentAutoBindTool\Scripts\Core\Editor\AutoBindPathUtility.cs ====================

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;

namespace ComponentAutoBindTool.Editor
{
    internal static class AutoBindPathUtility
    {
        private static bool WindowsPaths => Application.platform == RuntimePlatform.WindowsEditor;

        public static string GetResolvedCodePath(AutoBindGlobalSetting setting, Core.ComponentAutoBindTool target)
        {
            if (setting == null || target == null) return string.Empty;
            return NormalizeCodePath(setting.UseGlobalDefaultSavePath ? target.CodePath : setting.RawCodePath);
        }

        public static string GetResolvedCodePath(AutoBindGlobalSetting setting, string scriptAssetPath)
        {
            if (setting == null) return string.Empty;
            if (!setting.UseGlobalDefaultSavePath) return NormalizeCodePath(setting.RawCodePath);
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(scriptAssetPath)) return string.Empty;

            string scriptPath = CleanPath(scriptAssetPath);
            int separator = scriptPath.LastIndexOf('/');
            if (separator < 0) return string.Empty;
            string directory = scriptPath.Substring(0, separator);
            // MonoScript 路径是项目相对路径；Packages 不能被误加到 Assets 下。
            return NormalizeAbsolutePath(IsAbsolute(directory) ? directory : ProjectPath(Application.dataPath) + "/" + directory, WindowsPaths);
        }

        public static string GetInspectorCodePath(AutoBindGlobalSetting setting, string scriptAssetPath)
        {
            return ToAssetRelativePath(GetResolvedCodePath(setting, scriptAssetPath));
        }

        public static string NormalizeCodePath(string codePath)
        {
            return NormalizeCodePath(codePath, Application.dataPath, WindowsPaths);
        }

        // 只解释配置字符串。绝对路径检查和路径选择器不再进入这条兼容分支。
        internal static string NormalizeCodePath(string codePath, string assetsPath, bool windowsPaths)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(codePath)) return string.Empty;
            string assets = NormalizeAbsolutePath(assetsPath, windowsPaths);
            string path = CleanPath(codePath);
            StringComparison comparison = Comparison(windowsPaths);
            if (HasDrive(path) || path.StartsWith("//", StringComparison.Ordinal))
                return NormalizeAbsolutePath(path, windowsPaths);
            if (string.Equals(path, "Assets", comparison) || path.StartsWith("Assets/", comparison))
                return NormalizeAbsolutePath(assets + path.Substring("Assets".Length), windowsPaths);
            if (path.StartsWith("/", StringComparison.Ordinal))
            {
                string absolute = NormalizeAbsolutePath(path, windowsPaths);
                // 保留项目 Assets 绝对前缀及其近似前缀，再由严格边界检查拒绝 AssetsBackup。
                if (path.StartsWith(assets, comparison) || absolute.StartsWith(assets, comparison)) return absolute;
                path = path.TrimStart('/');
            }
            return NormalizeAbsolutePath(assets + "/" + path, windowsPaths);
        }

        public static bool IsPathUnderAssets(string path)
        {
            return IsPathUnderAssets(path, Application.dataPath, WindowsPaths);
        }

        internal static bool IsPathUnderAssets(string path, string assetsPath, bool windowsPaths)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(path)) return false;
            try
            {
                return IsWithin(NormalizeAbsolutePath(path, windowsPaths), NormalizeAbsolutePath(assetsPath, windowsPaths), Comparison(windowsPaths));
            }
            catch (ArgumentException)
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        public static string ToAssetRelativePath(string path)
        {
            return ToAssetRelativePath(path, Application.dataPath, WindowsPaths);
        }

        internal static string ToAssetRelativePath(string path, string assetsPath, bool windowsPaths)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(path)) return string.Empty;
            string absolute = NormalizeAbsolutePath(path, windowsPaths);
            string assets = NormalizeAbsolutePath(assetsPath, windowsPaths);
            return IsWithin(absolute, assets, Comparison(windowsPaths)) ? "Assets" + absolute.Substring(assets.Length) : absolute;
        }

        private static bool IsWithin(string path, string assets, StringComparison comparison)
        {
            return string.Equals(path, assets, comparison) || path.StartsWith(assets + "/", comparison);
        }

        private static StringComparison Comparison(bool windowsPaths)
        {
            return windowsPaths ? StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase : StringComparison.Ordinal;
        }

        private static string ProjectPath(string assetsPath)
        {
            string assets = NormalizeAbsolutePath(assetsPath, WindowsPaths);
            return assets.Substring(0, assets.LastIndexOf('/'));
        }

        private static string CleanPath(string path)
        {
            if (path.IndexOf('\0') >= 0) throw new ArgumentException("路径不能包含空字符。", nameof(path));
            return path.Trim().Replace('\\', '/');
        }

        private static bool HasDrive(string path)
        {
            return path.Length >= 2 && ((path[0] >= 'A' && path[0] <= 'Z') || (path[0] >= 'a' && path[0] <= 'z')) && path[1] == ':';
        }

        private static bool IsAbsolute(string path) => HasDrive(path) || path.StartsWith("/", StringComparison.Ordinal);

        // 词法规范化不依赖执行机器的 Path 规则，供 Windows 上的 Unix 规则测试使用。
        private static string NormalizeAbsolutePath(string input, bool windowsPaths)
        {
            string path = CleanPath(input);
            if (windowsPaths) ValidateWindowsCharacters(path);
            string root;
            string remainder;
            if (HasDrive(path))
            {
                if (path.Length < 3 || path[2] != '/') throw new ArgumentException("盘符路径必须是完整绝对路径。", nameof(input));
                root = path.Substring(0, 3);
                remainder = path.Substring(3);
            }
            else if (path.StartsWith("//", StringComparison.Ordinal))
            {
                string[] unc = path.Substring(2).Split(new[] { '/' }, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
                if (unc.Length < 2 || unc[0] == "." || unc[0] == ".." || unc[1] == "." || unc[1] == "..")
                    throw new ArgumentException("UNC 路径必须包含服务器和共享名称。", nameof(input));
                root = "//" + unc[0] + "/" + unc[1];
                remainder = string.Join("/", unc, 2, unc.Length - 2);
            }
            else if (path.StartsWith("/", StringComparison.Ordinal))
            {
                root = "/";
                remainder = path.Substring(1);
            }
            else throw new ArgumentException("此入口要求已经解析的绝对路径。", nameof(input));

            var segments = new List<string>();
            foreach (string segment in remainder.Split('/'))
            {
                if (segment.Length == 0 || segment == ".") continue;
                if (segment == "..")
                {
                    if (segments.Count > 0) segments.RemoveAt(segments.Count - 1);
                }
                else segments.Add(segment);
            }
            return segments.Count == 0 ? root : root.TrimEnd('/') + "/" + string.Join("/", segments);
        }

        private static void ValidateWindowsCharacters(string path)
        {
            for (int index = 0; index < path.Length; index++)
            {
                char character = path[index];
                bool driveSeparator = character == ':' && index == 1 && HasDrive(path);
                if (character < ' ' || character == '<' || character == '>' || character == '"' ||
                    character == '|' || character == '?' || character == '*' || character == ':' && !driveSeparator)
                    throw new ArgumentException("Windows 路径包含非法字符或备用数据流分隔符。", nameof(path));
            }
        }
    }
}
