using System;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEngine;
using BindData = ComponentAutoBindTool.Core.ComponentAutoBindTool.BindData;

namespace ComponentAutoBindTool.Editor
{
    internal static class AutoBindSerializedBindingUtility
    {
        public static bool ApplyCandidate(AutoBindBuildInput input, bool recordUndo = true)
        {
            var serialized = new SerializedObject(input.Target);
            var bindings = serialized.FindProperty("BindDatas");
            bool same = bindings.arraySize == input.Bindings.Count;
            for (int i = 0; same && i < bindings.arraySize; i++)
            {
                var item = bindings.GetArrayElementAtIndex(i);
                same = item.FindPropertyRelative("Name").stringValue == input.Bindings[i].Name &&
                       item.FindPropertyRelative("BindCom").objectReferenceValue == input.Bindings[i].BindCom;
            }
            AutoBindTargetScriptSyncUtility.ApplyMetadata(input, serialized);
            if (!same) RebuildBindDatas(bindings, input.Bindings);
            var components = serialized.FindProperty("m_BindComs");
            var names = serialized.FindProperty("m_BindFieldNames");
            if (NeedsRuntimeBindingSync(bindings, components, names))
                SyncRuntimeBindingCaches(bindings, components, names);
            return recordUndo ? serialized.ApplyModifiedProperties() : serialized.ApplyModifiedPropertiesWithoutUndo();
        }

        public static void AddBindData(SerializedProperty bindDatas, string name, Component bindCom)
        {
            int index = bindDatas.arraySize;
            bindDatas.InsertArrayElementAtIndex(index);

            SerializedProperty element = bindDatas.GetArrayElementAtIndex(index);
            element.FindPropertyRelative("Name").stringValue = name;
            element.FindPropertyRelative("BindCom").objectReferenceValue = bindCom;
        }

        public static void RebuildBindDatas(SerializedProperty bindDatas, System.Collections.Generic.IReadOnlyList<BindData> bindDataList)
        {
            bindDatas.ClearArray();
            for (int i = 0; i < bindDataList.Count; i++)
            {
                BindData bindData = bindDataList[i];
                AddBindData(bindDatas, bindData.Name, bindData.BindCom);
            }
        }

        public static void SyncRuntimeBindingCaches(SerializedProperty bindDatas, SerializedProperty bindComs,
            SerializedProperty bindFieldNames)
        {
            bindComs.ClearArray();
            bindFieldNames.ClearArray();

            for (int i = 0; i < bindDatas.arraySize; i++)
            {
                SerializedProperty bindDataElement = bindDatas.GetArrayElementAtIndex(i);
                SerializedProperty bindComponentProperty = bindDataElement.FindPropertyRelative("BindCom");
                SerializedProperty bindNameProperty = bindDataElement.FindPropertyRelative("Name");

                bindComs.InsertArrayElementAtIndex(i);
                bindComs.GetArrayElementAtIndex(i).objectReferenceValue = bindComponentProperty.objectReferenceValue;

                bindFieldNames.InsertArrayElementAtIndex(i);
                bindFieldNames.GetArrayElementAtIndex(i).stringValue =
                    AutoBindFieldNameUtility.BuildFieldName(bindNameProperty.stringValue);
            }
        }

        public static bool NeedsRuntimeBindingSync(SerializedProperty bindDatas, SerializedProperty bindComs,
            SerializedProperty bindFieldNames)
        {
            if (bindDatas == null || bindComs == null || bindFieldNames == null)
            {
                return false;
            }

            if (bindDatas.arraySize != bindComs.arraySize || bindDatas.arraySize != bindFieldNames.arraySize)
            {
                return true;
            }

            for (int i = 0; i < bindDatas.arraySize; i++)
            {
                SerializedProperty bindDataElement = bindDatas.GetArrayElementAtIndex(i);
                SerializedProperty bindNameProperty = bindDataElement.FindPropertyRelative("Name");
                SerializedProperty bindComponentProperty = bindDataElement.FindPropertyRelative("BindCom");

                SerializedProperty bindFieldNameProperty = bindFieldNames.GetArrayElementAtIndex(i);
                SerializedProperty bindComponentCacheProperty = bindComs.GetArrayElementAtIndex(i);

                string generatedFieldName = AutoBindFieldNameUtility.BuildFieldName(bindNameProperty.stringValue);
                if (!string.Equals(generatedFieldName, bindFieldNameProperty.stringValue, StringComparison.Ordinal))
                {
                    return true;
                }

                if (bindComponentProperty.objectReferenceValue != bindComponentCacheProperty.objectReferenceValue)
                {
                    return true;
                }
            }

            return false;
        }
    }
}


==================== FILE: E:\Github Unity Projects\VContainer ComponentAutoBindTool\Assets\Third Party\ComponentAutoBindTool\Scripts\Core\Editor\AutoBindSourceInspector.cs ====================

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
using UnityEditor.Compilation;

namespace ComponentAutoBindTool.Editor
{
    internal static class AutoBindSourceInspector
    {
        public static void ValidateDeclarations(AutoBindBuildInput input, AutoBindValidationResult result)
        {
            string assemblyName = Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(CompilationPipeline.GetAssemblyNameFromScriptPath(input.ScriptPath));
            var assembly = CompilationPipeline.GetAssemblies(AssembliesType.Editor).FirstOrDefault(a => a.name == assemblyName);
            IEnumerable<string> files = assembly != null ? assembly.sourceFiles : new[] { input.ScriptPath };
            bool found = false;
            foreach (string path in files)
            {
                if (!File.Exists(path)) continue;
                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(input.OutputPath) &&
                    string.Equals(Path.GetFullPath(path), Path.GetFullPath(input.OutputPath), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) continue;
                string source = File.ReadAllText(path);
                foreach (string declaration in FindDeclarations(source, input.HostType))
                {
                    found = true;
                    if (path.EndsWith(".BindComponents.cs", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    {
                        result.AddError("该宿主在其他位置已有生成文件，请先统一输出目录：" + path);
                        continue;
                    }
                    int open = declaration.IndexOf('{');
                    string header = declaration.Substring(0, open);
                    if (!Regex.IsMatch(header, @"\bpartial\b"))
                        result.AddError("目标脚本必须声明为 partial class：" + path);
                    foreach (string member in DirectMemberHeaders(declaration.Substring(open + 1)))
                    {
                        if (HasMemberConflict(member, source, input.HostType))
                            result.AddError("手写成员与 AutoBind 生成成员冲突：" + path + " → " + member.Trim());
                    }
                }
            }
            if (!found) result.AddError("无法定位目标脚本的 partial 类声明。");
        }

        private static bool HasMemberConflict(string member, string source, Type host)
        {
            string text = member.Replace("@", "").Trim();
            var getter = AutoBindCodeGenerator.FindBaseMethod(host, "GetGeneratedUIView");
            bool generatesGetter = getter != null && getter.ReturnType == typeof(object);
            bool Reserved(string name) => name == "view" || name == "UIView" ||
                name == "EnsureAutoBind" || (generatesGetter && name == "GetGeneratedUIView");
            var nestedType = Regex.Match(text, @"\b(class|struct|interface|enum|delegate)\s+(?:\S+\s+)?(\w+)");
            if (nestedType.Success) return Reserved(nestedType.Groups[2].Value);

            int parenthesis = text.IndexOf('(');
            string signature = parenthesis >= 0 ? text.Substring(0, parenthesis) : text;
            if (parenthesis >= 0 && !signature.Contains("="))
            {
                // 泛型方法和不同参数的重载不会与生成的非泛型方法冲突。
                var nameMatch = Regex.Match(signature, @"(\w+)\s*$");
                if (!nameMatch.Success) return false;
                string name = nameMatch.Groups[1].Value;
                if (name == "view" || name == "UIView") return true;
                int close = text.IndexOf(')', parenthesis);
                if (close < 0) return false;
                string parameters = text.Substring(parenthesis + 1, close - parenthesis - 1).Trim();
                if (name == "GetGeneratedUIView") return generatesGetter && parameters.Length == 0;
                if (name != "EnsureAutoBind" || parameters.Contains(",") ||
                    Regex.IsMatch(parameters, @"\b(ref|out|in)\b")) return false;
                var parameter = Regex.Match(parameters.Split('=')[0].Trim(), @"^(.+?)\s+(\w+)$");
                if (!parameter.Success) return false;
                string parameterType = Regex.Replace(parameter.Groups[1].Value, @"\s", "").Replace("global::", "");
                string aliasName = parameterType.Split('.')[0].Split(new[] { "::" }, StringSplitOptions.None)[0];
                var alias = Regex.Match(source, @"\busing\s+@?" + Regex.Escape(aliasName) + @"\s*=\s*([^;]+);");
                if (alias.Success)
                    parameterType = (Regex.Replace(alias.Groups[1].Value, @"\s", "") +
                        parameterType.Substring(aliasName.Length)).Replace("global::", "").Replace("::", ".");
                if (parameterType == "UnityEngine.GameObject") return true;
                if (parameterType != "GameObject") return false;
                // 利用已编译宿主消除同名 GameObject 类型造成的歧义。
                return host.GetMethods(System.Reflection.BindingFlags.Instance | System.Reflection.BindingFlags.Static |
                    System.Reflection.BindingFlags.Public | System.Reflection.BindingFlags.NonPublic |
                    System.Reflection.BindingFlags.DeclaredOnly).Any(method =>
                        !method.IsGenericMethod && (method.Name == name || method.Name.EndsWith("." + name, StringComparison.Ordinal)) &&
                        method.GetParameters().Length == 1 && method.GetParameters()[0].ParameterType == typeof(UnityEngine.GameObject) &&
                        method.GetParameters()[0].Name == parameter.Groups[2].Value);
            }

            // 分别检查每个变量声明，不让第一个初始化表达式截断后续字段。
            int depth = 0;
            int start = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i <= text.Length; i++)
            {
                char c = i == text.Length ? ',' : text[i];
                if (c == '(' || c == '[' || (c == '<' && IsTypeArgumentList(text.Substring(i)))) depth++;
                if (c == ')' || c == '>' || c == ']') depth = Math.Max(0, depth - 1);
                if (c != ',' || depth != 0) continue;
                string declarator = text.Substring(start, i - start).Split('=')[0].Trim();
                var name = Regex.Match(declarator, @"(\w+)\s*$");
                if (name.Success && Reserved(name.Groups[1].Value)) return true;
                start = i + 1;
            }
            return false;
        }

        private static bool IsTypeArgumentList(string text)
        {
            // 字段初始化中的比较/条件表达式不能把后续声明吞进“泛型参数”。
            return Regex.IsMatch(text, @"^<\s*(?:global\s*::\s*)?@?[\p{L}_](?:[\p{L}\p{N}_\s.,?<>\[\]]|::)*>");
        }

        // 仅做声明级词法识别：忽略注释、字符串、特性和方法体，避免将局部变量当作字段。
        internal static IEnumerable<string> FindDeclarations(string source, Type host)
        {
            string stripped = Regex.Replace(source,
                @"//[^\r\n]*|/\*[\s\S]*?\*/|@""(?:""""|[^""])*""|""(?:\\.|[^""\\])*""|'(?:\\.|[^'\\])*'", " ");
            string[] tokens = Regex.Matches(stripped, @"@?[\p{L}_][\p{L}\p{N}_]*|[^\s]")
                .Cast<Match>().Select(m => m.Value).ToArray();
            string currentNamespace = "";
            int depth = 0;
            var namespaceScopes = new Dictionary<int, string>();
            int headerStart = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < tokens.Length; i++)
            {
                string token = tokens[i];
                if (token == "namespace")
                {
                    int j = i + 1;
                    while (j < tokens.Length && tokens[j] != "{" && tokens[j] != ";") j++;
                    string suffix = string.Concat(tokens.Skip(i + 1).Take(j - i - 1)).Replace("@", "");
                    string previous = currentNamespace;
                    currentNamespace = currentNamespace.Length == 0 ? suffix : currentNamespace + "." + suffix;
                    if (j < tokens.Length && tokens[j] == "{")
                    {
                        depth++;
                        namespaceScopes[depth] = previous;
                    }
                    i = j;
                    headerStart = i + 1;
                    continue;
                }
                if (token == "class" && i + 1 < tokens.Length && tokens[i + 1].TrimStart('@') == host.Name &&
                    currentNamespace == (host.Namespace ?? "") && depth == namespaceScopes.Count)
                {
                    int open = i + 2;
                    while (open < tokens.Length && tokens[open] != "{") open++;
                    int end = open + 1;
                    int nested = 1;
                    while (end < tokens.Length && nested > 0)
                    {
                        if (tokens[end] == "{") nested++;
                        if (tokens[end] == "}") nested--;
                        end++;
                    }
                    if (nested == 0) yield return string.Join(" ", tokens.Skip(headerStart).Take(end - headerStart));
                    i = end - 1;
                    headerStart = end;
                    continue;
                }
                if (token == "{") { depth++; headerStart = i + 1; }
                if (token == "}")
                {
                    if (namespaceScopes.TryGetValue(depth, out string previous))
                    {
                        currentNamespace = previous;
                        namespaceScopes.Remove(depth);
                    }
                    depth--;
                    headerStart = i + 1;
                }
                if (token == ";") headerStart = i + 1;
            }
        }

        private static IEnumerable<string> DirectMemberHeaders(string body)
        {
            int depth = 0;
            int attributes = 0;
            var header = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
            foreach (char c in body)
            {
                if (depth == 0 && c == '[') attributes++;
                if (depth == 0 && attributes > 0)
                {
                    if (c == ']') attributes--;
                    continue;
                }
                if (c == '{')
                {
                    if (depth++ == 0) { yield return header.ToString(); header.Clear(); }
                    continue;
                }
                if (c == '}') { depth--; if (depth < 0) yield break; continue; }
                if (depth != 0) continue;
                if (c == ';') { yield return header.ToString(); header.Clear(); }
                else header.Append(c);
            }
        }
    }
}


==================== FILE: E:\Github Unity Projects\VContainer ComponentAutoBindTool\Assets\Third Party\ComponentAutoBindTool\Scripts\Core\Editor\AutoBindTargetScriptSyncUtility.cs ====================

using System;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEngine;

namespace ComponentAutoBindTool.Editor
{
    internal readonly struct AutoBindTargetScriptMetadata
    {
        public string Namespace { get; }
        public string ClassName { get; }
        public string CodePath { get; }

        public AutoBindTargetScriptMetadata(string namespaceValue, string className, string codePath)
        {
            Namespace = namespaceValue;
            ClassName = className;
            CodePath = codePath;
        }
    }

    internal static class AutoBindTargetScriptSyncUtility
    {
        internal static void ApplyMetadata(AutoBindBuildInput input, SerializedObject target)
        {
            target.FindProperty("m_Namespace").stringValue = input.HostType.Namespace ?? string.Empty;
            target.FindProperty("m_ClassName").stringValue = input.HostType.Name;
            target.FindProperty("m_CodePath").stringValue = input.CodePath;
        }

        public static bool SyncFromTargetScript(SerializedProperty targetScriptProperty, SerializedProperty namespaceProperty,
            SerializedProperty classNameProperty, SerializedProperty codePathProperty, AutoBindGlobalSetting setting)
        {
            bool succeeded = SyncFromTargetScript(targetScriptProperty, namespaceProperty, classNameProperty,
                codePathProperty, setting, out string error);
            if (!succeeded) Debug.LogError(error);
            return succeeded;
        }

        internal static bool SyncFromTargetScript(SerializedProperty targetScriptProperty, SerializedProperty namespaceProperty,
            SerializedProperty classNameProperty, SerializedProperty codePathProperty, AutoBindGlobalSetting setting, out string error)
        {
            error = null;
            if (targetScriptProperty == null || namespaceProperty == null || classNameProperty == null || codePathProperty == null)
            {
                error = "无法读取 AutoBind 元数据序列化字段。";
                return false;
            }

            var reference = targetScriptProperty.objectReferenceValue;
            if (reference != null && !(reference is MonoBehaviour))
            {
                error = "目标脚本不是有效的 MonoBehaviour。";
                return false;
            }
            if (!TryResolveMetadata(reference as MonoBehaviour, setting, out var metadata, out error)) return false;

            // 全部候选解析成功后才更新同一 SerializedObject，由调用方与引用变更一起提交。
            namespaceProperty.stringValue = metadata.Namespace;
            classNameProperty.stringValue = metadata.ClassName;
            codePathProperty.stringValue = metadata.CodePath;
            return true;
        }

        internal static bool TryResolveMetadata(MonoBehaviour targetScript, AutoBindGlobalSetting setting,
            out AutoBindTargetScriptMetadata metadata, out string error)
        {
            metadata = new AutoBindTargetScriptMetadata(string.Empty, string.Empty, string.Empty);
            error = null;
            if (targetScript == null) return true;
            if (setting == null)
            {
                error = "缺少 AutoBind 全局配置，无法解析目标脚本的输出路径。";
                return false;
            }
            try
            {
                MonoScript script = MonoScript.FromMonoBehaviour(targetScript);
                string scriptPath = script != null ? AssetDatabase.GetAssetPath(script) : string.Empty;
                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(scriptPath))
                {
                    error = "无法解析目标脚本的资源路径。";
                    return false;
                }
                string resolvedPath = AutoBindPathUtility.GetResolvedCodePath(setting, scriptPath);
                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(resolvedPath) || !AutoBindPathUtility.IsPathUnderAssets(resolvedPath))
                {
                    error = "代码保存路径必须是 Assets 内的有效目录：" + resolvedPath;
                    return false;
                }
                Type type = targetScript.GetType();
                metadata = new AutoBindTargetScriptMetadata(type.Namespace ?? string.Empty, type.Name,
                    AutoBindPathUtility.ToAssetRelativePath(resolvedPath));
                return true;
            }
            catch (Exception exception)
            {
                error = "无法解析 AutoBind 元数据：" + exception.Message;
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
}


==================== FILE: E:\Github Unity Projects\VContainer ComponentAutoBindTool\Assets\Third Party\ComponentAutoBindTool\Scripts\Core\Editor\AutoBindValidationResult.cs ====================

using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Text;

namespace ComponentAutoBindTool.Editor
{
    public sealed class AutoBindValidationResult
    {
        private readonly List<string> m_Errors = new List<string>();
        private readonly List<string> m_Warnings = new List<string>();

        public bool IsValid => m_Errors.Count == 0;
        public IReadOnlyList<string> Errors => m_Errors;
        public IReadOnlyList<string> Warnings => m_Warnings;
        public int ErrorCount => m_Errors.Count;
        public int WarningCount => m_Warnings.Count;

        public void AddError(string message)
        {
            m_Errors.Add(message);
        }

        public void AddWarning(string message)
        {
            m_Warnings.Add(message);
        }

        public string BuildReport()
        {
            var builder = new StringBuilder();
            bool hasPreviousSection = false;

            if (m_Errors.Count > 0)
            {
                builder.AppendLine("错误：");
                AppendMessages(builder, m_Errors);
                hasPreviousSection = true;
            }

            if (m_Warnings.Count > 0)
            {
                if (hasPreviousSection)
                {
                    builder.AppendLine();
                }

                builder.AppendLine("警告：");
                AppendMessages(builder, m_Warnings);
                hasPreviousSection = true;
            }

            if (!hasPreviousSection)
            {
                builder.Append("未发现问题。");
            }

            return builder.ToString();
        }

        private static void AppendMessages(StringBuilder builder, List<string> messages)
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < messages.Count; i++)
            {
                builder.Append("- ").AppendLine(messages[i]);
            }
        }
    }
}


==================== FILE: E:\Github Unity Projects\VContainer ComponentAutoBindTool\Assets\Third Party\ComponentAutoBindTool\Scripts\Core\Editor\AutoBindValidator.cs ====================

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using System.Reflection;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
using ComponentAutoBindTool.ViewCore;
using UnityEditor.Compilation;
using UnityEngine;
using BindData = ComponentAutoBindTool.Core.ComponentAutoBindTool.BindData;

namespace ComponentAutoBindTool.Editor
{
    internal static class AutoBindValidator
    {
        public static AutoBindValidationResult Validate(Core.ComponentAutoBindTool target, AutoBindGlobalSetting setting)
        {
            var result = new AutoBindValidationResult();
            if (target == null || setting == null)
            {
                result.AddError("AutoBind 目标或全局配置为空。");
                return result;
            }
            try
            {
                Validate(new AutoBindBuildInput(target, setting, target.BindDatas), result);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                result.AddError("无法准备校验输入：" + ex.Message);
            }
            return result;
        }

        internal static void Validate(AutoBindBuildInput input, AutoBindValidationResult result)
        {
            ValidateHost(input, result);
            ValidatePath(input, result);
            ValidateBindings(input, result);
        }

        private static void ValidateHost(AutoBindBuildInput input, AutoBindValidationResult result)
        {
            Type type = input.HostType;
            if (type == null)
            {
                result.AddError("未指定目标脚本。");
                return;
            }
            if (input.Target.m_targetScript.gameObject != input.Target.gameObject)
                result.AddError("目标脚本必须挂在当前 AutoBind 根节点上。");
            if (type.IsAbstract || type.IsGenericType || type.IsNested)
                result.AddError("目标脚本必须是非抽象、非泛型、非嵌套的 MonoBehaviour。");
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(input.ScriptPath) || !File.Exists(input.ScriptPath))
            {
                result.AddError("无法读取目标脚本源码。");
                return;
            }

            AutoBindSourceInspector.ValidateDeclarations(input, result);
            MethodInfo ensure = AutoBindCodeGenerator.FindBaseMethod(type, "EnsureAutoBind", typeof(GameObject));
            if (ensure != null && !AutoBindCodeGenerator.CanOverride(ensure, typeof(void)))
                result.AddError("最近的基类 EnsureAutoBind 不是可访问、未封闭的 void 虚方法。");
            MethodInfo getter = AutoBindCodeGenerator.FindBaseMethod(type, "GetGeneratedUIView");
            bool requiresGetter = typeof(WidgetViewBase).IsAssignableFrom(type) ||
                                  (getter != null && getter.ReturnType == typeof(object));
            if (requiresGetter)
            {
                if (!AutoBindCodeGenerator.CanOverride(getter, typeof(object)))
                    result.AddError("GetGeneratedUIView 必须是可访问、未封闭的 object 虚方法。");
            }
        }

        private static void ValidatePath(AutoBindBuildInput input, AutoBindValidationResult result)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(input.OutputPath))
            {
                result.AddError("无法解析代码保存路径。");
                return;
            }
            if (!AutoBindPathUtility.IsPathUnderAssets(input.OutputPath))
            {
                result.AddError("代码保存路径必须位于 Assets 内：" + input.OutputPath);
                return;
            }
            string outputAssetPath = AutoBindPathUtility.ToAssetRelativePath(input.OutputPath);
            string sourceAssembly = CompilationPipeline.GetAssemblyNameFromScriptPath(input.ScriptPath);
            string outputAssembly = CompilationPipeline.GetAssemblyNameFromScriptPath(outputAssetPath);
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sourceAssembly) || sourceAssembly != outputAssembly)
                result.AddError("生成文件必须与目标脚本位于同一程序集，请开启跟随目标脚本路径。");
            if (File.Exists(input.OutputPath))
            {
                string existing = File.ReadAllText(input.OutputPath);
                string marker = "// AutoBind host: " + input.HostType?.FullName + "\n";
                bool owned = existing.Replace("\r\n", "\n").Contains(marker);
                // 接纳此前生成器留下的无固定文件头产物。
                bool legacy = existing.Contains("//自动生成于：") &&
                    Regex.IsMatch(existing, @"\bpartial\s+class\s+" + Regex.Escape(input.HostType?.Name ?? "") + @"\b") &&
                    (string.IsNullOrEmpty(input.HostType?.Namespace)
                        ? !Regex.IsMatch(existing, @"\bnamespace\s+")
                        : Regex.IsMatch(existing, @"\bnamespace\s+" + Regex.Escape(input.HostType.Namespace) + @"\s*\{"));
                if (!owned && !legacy) result.AddError("输出文件已由其他宿主或手写代码占用：" + outputAssetPath);
            }
        }

        private static void ValidateBindings(AutoBindBuildInput input, AutoBindValidationResult result)
        {
            IReadOnlyList<BindData> bindings = input.Bindings;
            if (bindings == null || bindings.Count == 0)
            {
                result.AddWarning("当前没有绑定数据，生成的 UIView 将为空。");
                return;
            }
            var names = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
            for (int i = 0; i < bindings.Count; i++)
            {
                BindData binding = bindings[i];
                if (binding == null)
                {
                    result.AddError($"[{i}] 绑定记录为空。");
                    continue;
                }
                string name = AutoBindFieldNameUtility.BuildFieldName(binding.Name);
                if (!AutoBindFieldNameUtility.IsValidIdentifier(name) || name == "UIView")
                    result.AddError($"[{i}] 生成字段名非法或与类型名冲突：{name}");
                else if (!names.Add(name))
                    result.AddError($"[{i}] 生成字段名重复：{name}");
                if (binding.BindCom == null)
                {
                    result.AddError($"[{i}] 组件引用为空。");
                    continue;
                }
                Transform transform = binding.BindCom.transform;
                if (transform != input.Target.transform && !transform.IsChildOf(input.Target.transform))
                    result.AddError($"[{i}] 组件不属于当前 AutoBind 根节点。");
                Type type = binding.BindCom.GetType();
                if (type.ContainsGenericParameters || !type.IsVisible)
                    result.AddError($"[{i}] 组件类型不可被生成代码公开引用：{type.FullName}");
                if (binding.BindCom == input.Target.m_targetScript)
                    result.AddError($"[{i}] 不能将当前根节点的目标脚本自身作为绑定项。");
                if (binding.Name != null && binding.Name.StartsWith("Widget_", StringComparison.Ordinal) &&
                    binding.BindCom is not WidgetViewBase)
                    result.AddError($"[{i}] Widget 前缀绑定的组件必须继承 WidgetViewBase：{type.FullName}");
                if (binding.Name != null && binding.Name.StartsWith("Sub_", StringComparison.Ordinal))
                {
                    bool hasNestedTarget = binding.BindCom.TryGetComponent<Core.ComponentAutoBindTool>(out var nested) &&
                                           nested.m_targetScript == binding.BindCom && nested != binding.BindCom;
                    if (binding.BindCom.gameObject == input.Target.gameObject ||
                        (!(binding.BindCom is IAutoBindHost) && !hasNestedTarget))
                        result.AddError($"[{i}] Sub 前缀必须绑定嵌套 AutoBind 子宿主脚本：{type.FullName}");
                }
            }
        }

    }
}


==================== FILE: E:\Github Unity Projects\VContainer ComponentAutoBindTool\Assets\Third Party\ComponentAutoBindTool\Scripts\Core\Editor\ComponentAutoBindToolInspector.cs ====================

using ComponentAutoBindTool.Core;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEngine;

namespace ComponentAutoBindTool.Editor
{
    [CustomEditor(typeof(Core.ComponentAutoBindTool), true)]
    public class ComponentAutoBindToolInspector : UnityEditor.Editor
    {
        private Core.ComponentAutoBindTool m_Target;
        private AutoBindGlobalSetting m_Setting;
        private AutoBindKeyMapSetting m_KeyMapSetting;
        private SerializedProperty m_BindDatas;
        private SerializedProperty m_BindComs;
        private SerializedProperty m_BindFieldNames;
        private SerializedProperty m_TargetScript;
        private bool m_ShowBindings;
        private bool m_ShowKeyMap;
        private string m_LastReport;
        private string m_MetadataError;

        private void OnEnable()
        {
            m_Target = (Core.ComponentAutoBindTool)target;
            m_BindDatas = serializedObject.FindProperty("BindDatas");
            m_BindComs = serializedObject.FindProperty("m_BindComs");
            m_BindFieldNames = serializedObject.FindProperty("m_BindFieldNames");
            m_TargetScript = serializedObject.FindProperty("m_targetScript");
            LoadSettings();
        }

        private void LoadSettings()
        {
            m_Setting = AutoBindEditorAssetLocator.LoadSingletonAsset<AutoBindGlobalSetting>();
            m_KeyMapSetting = AutoBindEditorAssetLocator.LoadSingletonAsset<AutoBindKeyMapSetting>();
        }

        public override void OnInspectorGUI()
        {
            serializedObject.Update();
            EditorGUI.BeginChangeCheck();
            EditorGUILayout.PropertyField(m_TargetScript, new GUIContent("脚本"));
            if (EditorGUI.EndChangeCheck()) ApplyTargetScriptChange(m_Setting, out _);
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(m_MetadataError)) EditorGUILayout.HelpBox(m_MetadataError, MessageType.Error);

            // 摘要始终取实际脚本与当前配置；重绘不会把候选写回组件。
            if (AutoBindTargetScriptSyncUtility.TryResolveMetadata(m_Target.m_targetScript, m_Setting,
                out var metadata, out string previewError))
                AutoBindInspectorDrawer.DrawSettingsSection(metadata.Namespace, metadata.ClassName, metadata.CodePath);
            else if (m_Setting != null)
                EditorGUILayout.HelpBox(previewError, MessageType.Error);

            if (m_Setting == null || m_KeyMapSetting == null)
            {
                EditorGUILayout.HelpBox("缺少唯一的 AutoBind 全局配置或组件映射配置，请修复配置后重试。", MessageType.Error);
                if (GUILayout.Button("重新加载配置")) LoadSettings();
                return;
            }
            using (new EditorGUI.DisabledScope(EditorApplication.isPlayingOrWillChangePlaymode))
            {
                AutoBindInspectorDrawer.DrawActionSection(HandleAutoBindAndValidate, HandleGenerateCode, HandleRemoveAll);
            }
            AutoBindInspectorDrawer.DrawBindComponentFoldout(m_BindDatas, ref m_ShowBindings, DrawBindings);
            AutoBindInspectorDrawer.DrawKeyMapTip(m_KeyMapSetting, ref m_ShowKeyMap);
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(m_LastReport)) EditorGUILayout.HelpBox(m_LastReport, MessageType.None);
        }

        internal bool ApplyTargetScriptChange(AutoBindGlobalSetting setting, out string error)
        {
            if (!AutoBindTargetScriptSyncUtility.SyncFromTargetScript(m_TargetScript,
                serializedObject.FindProperty("m_Namespace"), serializedObject.FindProperty("m_ClassName"),
                serializedObject.FindProperty("m_CodePath"), setting, out error))
            {
                serializedObject.Update();
                m_MetadataError = "此次脚本选择未应用：" + error;
                return false;
            }
            serializedObject.ApplyModifiedProperties();
            m_MetadataError = null;
            return true;
        }

        private void DrawBindings()
        {
            EditorGUI.BeginChangeCheck();
            int index = AutoBindInspectorDrawer.DrawBindDataList(m_BindDatas);
            if (index >= 0)
            {
                m_BindDatas.DeleteArrayElementAtIndex(index);
                GUI.changed = true;
            }
            if (EditorGUI.EndChangeCheck())
            {
                AutoBindSerializedBindingUtility.SyncRuntimeBindingCaches(m_BindDatas, m_BindComs, m_BindFieldNames);
                serializedObject.ApplyModifiedProperties();
            }
        }

        private void HandleRemoveAll()
        {
            m_BindDatas.ClearArray();
            m_BindComs.ClearArray();
            m_BindFieldNames.ClearArray();
            serializedObject.ApplyModifiedProperties();
        }

        private void HandleAutoBindAndValidate() => RunPipeline(false);
        private void HandleGenerateCode() => RunPipeline(true);

        internal AutoBindPipelineReport RunPipeline(bool generate, bool showDialog = true,
            AutoBindGlobalSetting settingsOverride = null, AutoBindKeyMapSetting keyMapOverride = null)
        {
            serializedObject.ApplyModifiedProperties();
            AutoBindPipelineReport report = generate
                ? AutoBindBuildPipeline.GenerateCode(m_Target, settingsOverride ?? m_Setting, keyMapOverride ?? m_KeyMapSetting)
                : AutoBindBuildPipeline.RescanAndValidate(m_Target, settingsOverride ?? m_Setting, keyMapOverride ?? m_KeyMapSetting);
            serializedObject.Update();
            string title = report.Succeeded ? (generate ? "生成完成" : "校验通过") : "操作失败";
            m_LastReport = title + "\n" + report.ValidationResult.BuildReport();
            if (generate && report.Succeeded)
                m_LastReport += report.CodeFileUpdated ? "\n已保存所属场景或 Prefab，并更新代码。" : "\n代码内容未变化，已跳过写入。";
            if (report.Succeeded)
            {
                if (report.ValidationResult.WarningCount > 0) Debug.LogWarning(m_LastReport, m_Target);
                else Debug.Log(m_LastReport, m_Target);
            }
            else Debug.LogError(m_LastReport, m_Target);
            if (showDialog && !Application.isBatchMode) EditorUtility.DisplayDialog(title, m_LastReport, "确定");
            return report;
        }
    }
}

#if UNITY_EDITOR
using ComponentAutoBindTool.Core;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEngine;

namespace ComponentAutoBindTool.Editor
{
    public class CustomContextMenu
    {
        [MenuItem("CONTEXT/MonoBehaviour/Add AutoBindComponentTool", false, 0)]
        private static void AddAutoBindComponentTool(MenuCommand command)
        {
            // 获取右键点击的对象
            var mono = command.context as MonoBehaviour;
            if (mono != null)
            {
                var selectedObject = mono.gameObject;

                // 检查是否已经挂载了 MyScript
                if (selectedObject != null)
                    if (selectedObject.GetComponent<Core.ComponentAutoBindTool>() == null)
                    {
                        // 挂载脚本
                        var com = selectedObject.AddComponent<Core.ComponentAutoBindTool>();
                        com.m_targetScript = mono;
                        Debug.Log("MyScript has been added to: " + selectedObject.name);
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        Debug.LogWarning("MyScript is already attached to: " + selectedObject.name);
                    }
            }
        }
        
        
        // 添加一个方法来控制菜单项的可用性
        [MenuItem("CONTEXT/MonoBehaviour/Add AutoBindComponentTool", true)]
        private static bool ValidateAddAutoBindComponentTool(MenuCommand command)
        {
            var mono = command.context as MonoBehaviour;
            // 如果当前选中的 MonoBehaviour 是 ComponentAutoBindTool，则返回 false
            return mono != null && !(mono is Core.ComponentAutoBindTool);
        }
        
        
        
        
        
        [MenuItem("CONTEXT/MonoBehaviour/Add Component To AutoBindKeyMapSetting", false, 0)]
        private static void AddComponentToAutoBindKeyMapSetting(MenuCommand command)
        {
            // 获取右键点击的对象
            var mono = command.context as MonoBehaviour;
            if (mono != null)
            {
                // 获取当前字符串中 每个大写 的字母
                var str = "";
                var type = mono.GetType();
                var typeName = type.FullName;
                foreach (var t in type.Name)
                {
                    if (char.IsUpper(t))
                    {
                        str += t;
                    }
                }

                var lowerStr = str.ToLower();
                Debug.Log($"{typeName}, 缩写:{lowerStr}");
                
                var autoBindKeyMapInputWizard = ScriptableWizard.DisplayWizard<AutoBindKeyMapInputWizard>("缩写前缀和类型名", "确定");
                autoBindKeyMapInputWizard.key = lowerStr;
                autoBindKeyMapInputWizard.typeName = typeName;
                autoBindKeyMapInputWizard.minSize = new Vector2(300, 200);
                autoBindKeyMapInputWizard.maxSize = new Vector2(300, 200);
            }
        }
        
        
        // 添加一个方法来控制菜单项的可用性
        [MenuItem("CONTEXT/MonoBehaviour/Add Component To AutoBindKeyMapSetting", true)]
        private static bool ValidateAddComponentToAutoBindKeyMapSetting(MenuCommand command)
        {
            var mono = command.context as MonoBehaviour;
            // 如果当前选中的 MonoBehaviour 是 ComponentAutoBindTool，则返回 false
            return mono != null && !(mono is Core.ComponentAutoBindTool);
        }
    }
}

==================== FILE: E:\Github Unity Projects\VContainer ComponentAutoBindTool\Assets\Third Party\ComponentAutoBindTool\Scripts\Core\Editor\ViewMonitorWindow.cs ====================

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using ComponentAutoBindTool.ViewCore;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UIElements;

namespace ComponentAutoBindTool.Editor
{
    public sealed class ViewMonitorWindow : EditorWindow
    {
        private static readonly UILayer[] s_DisplayLayers =
        {
            UILayer.System, UILayer.Top, UILayer.Normal, UILayer.HUD
        };

        private readonly List<IViewManagerDiagnostics> m_Managers = new();
        private readonly List<string> m_ManagerChoices = new() { "无活跃管理器" };
        private readonly List<WindowDiagnosticSnapshot> m_WindowBuffer = new(32);
        private readonly List<QueuedRequestSnapshot> m_QueueBuffer = new(16);
        private readonly List<Label> m_QueueRows = new();
        private readonly WindowSection[] m_LayerSections = new WindowSection[4];
        private IViewManagerDiagnostics m_SelectedManager;
        private PopupField<string> m_ManagerSelector;
        private Label m_Summary;
        private VisualElement m_Actions;
        private WindowSection m_InactiveSection;
        private Box m_QueueSection;
        private Label m_QueueHeader;
        private Label m_QueueEmpty;
        private double m_NextRefreshTime;
        private bool m_IsChangingPlayMode;

        [MenuItem("自动绑定UI组件/框架实验台/运行期窗口监控台")]
        public static void Open()
        {
            var window = GetWindow<ViewMonitorWindow>("窗口运行监控台");
            window.minSize = new Vector2(520, 420);
        }

        private void OnEnable()
        {
            EditorApplication.update += OnEditorUpdate;
            EditorApplication.playModeStateChanged += OnPlayModeStateChanged;
        }

        private void OnDisable()
        {
            EditorApplication.update -= OnEditorUpdate;
            EditorApplication.playModeStateChanged -= OnPlayModeStateChanged;
            ClearReferences();
        }

        public void CreateGUI()
        {
            ClearReferences();
            rootVisualElement.Clear();
            rootVisualElement.styleSheets.Clear();
            m_QueueRows.Clear();
            string scriptPath = AssetDatabase.GetAssetPath(MonoScript.FromScriptableObject(this));
            string stylePath = Path.Combine(Path.GetDirectoryName(scriptPath) ?? string.Empty,
                "ViewMonitorWindow.uss").Replace('\\', '/');
            var styleSheet = AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<StyleSheet>(stylePath);
            if (styleSheet != null)
            {
                rootVisualElement.styleSheets.Add(styleSheet);
            }

            rootVisualElement.AddToClassList("view-monitor");
            rootVisualElement.Add(CreateLabel("VIEWCORE / 运行期窗口监控台", "monitor-title"));
            m_ManagerSelector = new PopupField<string>("管理器", m_ManagerChoices, 0)
            {
                name = "managerSelector"
            };
            m_ManagerSelector.RegisterValueChangedCallback(evt =>
            {
                int index = m_ManagerChoices.IndexOf(evt.newValue);
                m_SelectedManager = index >= 0 && index < m_Managers.Count ? m_Managers[index] : null;
                RefreshView();
            });
            rootVisualElement.Add(m_ManagerSelector);
            m_Summary = CreateLabel(string.Empty, "monitor-summary");
            m_Summary.name = "summary";
            rootVisualElement.Add(m_Summary);
            m_Actions = new VisualElement { name = "actions" };
            m_Actions.AddToClassList("monitor-actions");
            m_Actions.Add(CreateButton("导航返回", "navigateBack", () => Execute(manager => manager.TryNavigateBack())));
            m_Actions.Add(CreateButton("关闭全部活动窗口", "closeAll", () => Execute(manager => manager.CloseAllViews())));
            m_Actions.Add(CreateButton("关闭全部并销毁缓存", "destroyAll", () => Execute(manager => manager.CloseAllViews(true))));
            m_Actions.Add(CreateButton("清空等待队列", "clearQueue", () => Execute(manager => manager.ClearWaitingQueue())));
            rootVisualElement.Add(m_Actions);

            var scroll = new ScrollView(ScrollViewMode.Vertical) { name = "content" };
            scroll.AddToClassList("monitor-scroll");
            rootVisualElement.Add(scroll);
            for (int i = 0; i < s_DisplayLayers.Length; i++)
            {
                m_LayerSections[i] = new WindowSection(this, "layer" + s_DisplayLayers[i],
                    "层级 " + s_DisplayLayers[i]);
                scroll.Add(m_LayerSections[i].Root);
            }

            m_QueueSection = new Box { name = "queue" };
            m_QueueSection.AddToClassList("monitor-section");
            m_QueueHeader = CreateLabel("等待队列", "monitor-header");
            m_QueueEmpty = new Label("当前没有排队请求。");
            m_QueueSection.Add(m_QueueHeader);
            m_QueueSection.Add(m_QueueEmpty);
            scroll.Add(m_QueueSection);
            m_InactiveSection = new WindowSection(this, "inactive", "缓存与待销毁池");
            scroll.Add(m_InactiveSection.Root);
            RefreshView();
        }

        private void OnEditorUpdate()
        {
            if (EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup < m_NextRefreshTime)
            {
                return;
            }

            m_NextRefreshTime = EditorApplication.timeSinceStartup + 0.25d;
            RefreshView();
        }

        private void OnPlayModeStateChanged(PlayModeStateChange state)
        {
            m_IsChangingPlayMode = state == PlayModeStateChange.ExitingEditMode ||
                                   state == PlayModeStateChange.ExitingPlayMode;
            ClearReferences();
            RefreshView();
        }

        internal void RefreshView()
        {
            if (m_Summary == null)
            {
                return;
            }

            if (!EditorApplication.isPlaying || m_IsChangingPlayMode)
            {
                ClearReferences();
                m_Summary.text = "请进入 Play Mode，以监控窗口、缓存与等待队列。";
                return;
            }

            RefreshManagers();
            var manager = GetActiveManager();
            m_Actions.SetEnabled(manager != null);
            if (manager == null)
            {
                ClearRows();
                m_Summary.text = "当前没有活跃的 ViewManager 实例。";
                return;
            }

            m_Summary.text = $"活跃管理器：{m_ManagerChoices.IndexOf(m_ManagerSelector.value) + 1}/{m_Managers.Count}  |  " +
                             $"总登记实例：{manager.TotalInstanceCount}  |  等待队列：{manager.WaitingQueueCount}";
            for (int i = 0; i < s_DisplayLayers.Length; i++)
            {
                m_WindowBuffer.Clear();
                manager.GetLayerSnapshots(s_DisplayLayers[i], m_WindowBuffer);
                m_LayerSections[i].Bind(manager, m_WindowBuffer, true);
            }

            m_WindowBuffer.Clear();
            manager.GetInactiveSnapshots(m_WindowBuffer);
            m_InactiveSection.Bind(manager, m_WindowBuffer, false);
            m_WindowBuffer.Clear();
            m_QueueBuffer.Clear();
            manager.GetWaitingQueueSnapshots(m_QueueBuffer);
            m_QueueHeader.text = $"等待队列 — {m_QueueBuffer.Count} 项";
            m_QueueEmpty.EnableInClassList("monitor-hidden", m_QueueBuffer.Count != 0);
            for (int i = 0; i < m_QueueBuffer.Count; i++)
            {
                if (i == m_QueueRows.Count)
                {
                    var row = CreateLabel(string.Empty, "monitor-queue-row");
                    m_QueueRows.Add(row);
                    m_QueueSection.Add(row);
                }

                var request = m_QueueBuffer[i];
                m_QueueRows[i].text = $"[{request.QueueIndex}] {request.ViewName}  |  优先级：{request.Priority}  |  参数：{request.ArgsTypeName}";
                m_QueueRows[i].RemoveFromClassList("monitor-hidden");
            }

            for (int i = m_QueueBuffer.Count; i < m_QueueRows.Count; i++)
            {
                m_QueueRows[i].text = string.Empty;
                m_QueueRows[i].AddToClassList("monitor-hidden");
            }
            m_QueueBuffer.Clear();
        }

        private void RefreshManagers()
        {
            var managers = ViewManagerDebugRegistry.ActiveManagers;
            bool changed = managers.Count != m_Managers.Count;
            for (int i = 0; !changed && i < managers.Count; i++)
            {
                changed = !ReferenceEquals(managers[i], m_Managers[i]);
            }

            if (!changed)
            {
                return;
            }

            m_Managers.Clear();
            m_ManagerChoices.Clear();
            int selectedIndex = -1;
            for (int i = 0; i < managers.Count; i++)
            {
                m_Managers.Add(managers[i]);
                m_ManagerChoices.Add($"{managers[i].GetType().Name} #{i + 1}");
                if (ReferenceEquals(m_SelectedManager, managers[i]))
                {
                    selectedIndex = i;
                }
            }

            if (m_Managers.Count == 0)
            {
                m_SelectedManager = null;
                m_ManagerChoices.Add("无活跃管理器");
            }
            else
            {
                selectedIndex = Math.Max(0, selectedIndex);
                m_SelectedManager = m_Managers[selectedIndex];
            }

            m_ManagerSelector.choices = m_ManagerChoices;
            m_ManagerSelector.SetValueWithoutNotify(m_ManagerChoices[Math.Max(0, selectedIndex)]);
            m_ManagerSelector.SetEnabled(m_Managers.Count > 1);
        }

        private IViewManagerDiagnostics GetActiveManager()
        {
            if (!EditorApplication.isPlaying || m_IsChangingPlayMode || m_SelectedManager == null || m_SelectedManager.IsDisposed)
            {
                return null;
            }

            var managers = ViewManagerDebugRegistry.ActiveManagers;
            for (int i = 0; i < managers.Count; i++)
            {
                if (ReferenceEquals(managers[i], m_SelectedManager))
                {
                    return m_SelectedManager;
                }
            }

            return null;
        }

        private void Execute(Action<IViewManagerDiagnostics> action)
        {
            try
            {
                var manager = GetActiveManager();
                if (manager != null)
                {
                    action(manager);
                }
            }
            catch (Exception exception)
            {
                Debug.LogException(exception);
            }
            finally
            {
                RefreshView();
            }
        }

        private void ExecuteRow(IViewManagerDiagnostics manager, int instanceId, bool close)
        {
            Execute(activeManager =>
            {
                if (!ReferenceEquals(activeManager, manager))
                {
                    return;
                }

                for (int i = 0; i <= s_DisplayLayers.Length; i++)
                {
                    m_WindowBuffer.Clear();
                    if (i == s_DisplayLayers.Length)
                    {
                        activeManager.GetInactiveSnapshots(m_WindowBuffer);
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        activeManager.GetLayerSnapshots(s_DisplayLayers[i], m_WindowBuffer);
                    }

                    for (int j = 0; j < m_WindowBuffer.Count; j++)
                    {
                        var snapshot = m_WindowBuffer[j];
                        if (snapshot.InstanceId != instanceId)
                        {
                            continue;
                        }

                        m_WindowBuffer.Clear();
                        if (close)
                        {
                            if (CanClose(snapshot.State))
                            {
                                activeManager.CloseView(instanceId);
                            }
                        }
                        else if (snapshot.HostObject != null)
                        {
                            Selection.activeObject = snapshot.HostObject;
                            EditorGUIUtility.PingObject(snapshot.HostObject);
                        }
                        return;
                    }
                }
                m_WindowBuffer.Clear();
            });
        }

        private void ClearReferences()
        {
            m_SelectedManager = null;
            m_Managers.Clear();
            m_ManagerChoices.Clear();
            m_ManagerChoices.Add("无活跃管理器");
            if (m_ManagerSelector != null)
            {
                m_ManagerSelector.choices = m_ManagerChoices;
                m_ManagerSelector.SetValueWithoutNotify(m_ManagerChoices[0]);
                m_ManagerSelector.SetEnabled(false);
            }

            m_Actions?.SetEnabled(false);
            ClearRows();
        }

        private void ClearRows()
        {
            m_WindowBuffer.Clear();
            m_QueueBuffer.Clear();
            for (int i = 0; i < m_LayerSections.Length; i++)
            {
                m_LayerSections[i]?.Clear();
            }
            m_InactiveSection?.Clear();
            for (int i = 0; i < m_QueueRows.Count; i++)
            {
                m_QueueRows[i].text = string.Empty;
                m_QueueRows[i].AddToClassList("monitor-hidden");
            }
            if (m_QueueHeader != null)
            {
                m_QueueHeader.text = "等待队列 — 0 项";
                m_QueueEmpty.RemoveFromClassList("monitor-hidden");
            }
        }

        private static bool CanClose(WindowState state)
        {
            return state == WindowState.OpenAndActive || state == WindowState.Paused;
        }

        private static Label CreateLabel(string text, string className)
        {
            var label = new Label(text);
            label.AddToClassList(className);
            return label;
        }

        private static Button CreateButton(string text, string name, Action action)
        {
            return new Button(action) { text = text, name = name };
        }

        private sealed class WindowSection
        {
            public readonly Box Root;
            private readonly ViewMonitorWindow m_Window;
            private readonly string m_Title;
            private readonly Label m_Header;
            private readonly Label m_Empty;
            private readonly List<WindowRow> m_Rows = new();

            public WindowSection(ViewMonitorWindow window, string name, string title)
            {
                m_Window = window;
                m_Title = title;
                Root = new Box { name = name };
                Root.AddToClassList("monitor-section");
                m_Header = CreateLabel(title, "monitor-header");
                m_Empty = new Label("当前没有窗口。");
                Root.Add(m_Header);
                Root.Add(m_Empty);
            }

            public void Bind(IViewManagerDiagnostics manager, List<WindowDiagnosticSnapshot> snapshots, bool reverse)
            {
                m_Header.text = $"{m_Title} — {snapshots.Count} 项";
                m_Empty.EnableInClassList("monitor-hidden", snapshots.Count != 0);
                for (int i = 0; i < snapshots.Count; i++)
                {
                    if (i == m_Rows.Count)
                    {
                        var row = new WindowRow(m_Window);
                        m_Rows.Add(row);
                        Root.Add(row.Root);
                    }
                    m_Rows[i].Bind(manager, snapshots[reverse ? snapshots.Count - i - 1 : i], reverse && i == 0);
                }
                for (int i = snapshots.Count; i < m_Rows.Count; i++)
                {
                    m_Rows[i].Clear();
                }
            }

            public void Clear()
            {
                m_Header.text = m_Title + " — 0 项";
                m_Empty.RemoveFromClassList("monitor-hidden");
                for (int i = 0; i < m_Rows.Count; i++)
                {
                    m_Rows[i].Clear();
                }
            }
        }

        private sealed class WindowRow
        {
            public readonly VisualElement Root = new();
            private readonly Label m_Info;
            private readonly Button m_Ping;
            private readonly Button m_Close;
            private IViewManagerDiagnostics m_Manager;
            private int m_InstanceId;

            public WindowRow(ViewMonitorWindow window)
            {
                Root.AddToClassList("monitor-row");
                m_Info = CreateLabel(string.Empty, "monitor-row-info");
                m_Ping = CreateButton("定位", "ping", () => window.ExecuteRow(m_Manager, m_InstanceId, false));
                m_Close = CreateButton("关闭", "close", () => window.ExecuteRow(m_Manager, m_InstanceId, true));
                Root.Add(m_Info);
                Root.Add(m_Ping);
                Root.Add(m_Close);
            }

            public void Bind(IViewManagerDiagnostics manager, WindowDiagnosticSnapshot snapshot, bool top)
            {
                m_Manager = manager;
                m_InstanceId = snapshot.InstanceId;
                string timer = snapshot.State == WindowState.PendingDestroy
                    ? $" · 销毁倒计时 {snapshot.RemainingDestroyTime:F1}s" : string.Empty;
                string transition = snapshot.IsTransitioning ? " · 过渡中" : string.Empty;
                m_Info.text = $"#{snapshot.InstanceId} {(top ? "[栈顶] " : string.Empty)}{snapshot.ViewName} [{snapshot.State}]{transition}\n" +
                              $"Order:{snapshot.SortingOrder} · 子Canvas:{snapshot.SubCanvasCount} · " +
                              $"{snapshot.InstanceMode}/{snapshot.CoverMode}/{snapshot.CloseStrategy} · " +
                              $"允许返回:{snapshot.AllowNavigateBack}{timer}";
                m_Ping.SetEnabled(snapshot.HostObject != null);
                m_Close.SetEnabled(CanClose(snapshot.State));
                Root.RemoveFromClassList("monitor-hidden");
            }

            public void Clear()
            {
                m_Manager = null;
                m_InstanceId = 0;
                m_Info.text = string.Empty;
                m_Ping.SetEnabled(false);
                m_Close.SetEnabled(false);
                Root.AddToClassList("monitor-hidden");
            }
        }
    }
}

==================== FILE: E:\Github Unity Projects\VContainer ComponentAutoBindTool\Assets\Third Party\ComponentAutoBindTool\Scripts\ViewCore\Contracts\IViewManager.cs ====================

using System;
using System.Threading;
using UnityEngine;

// ReSharper disable once CheckNamespace
namespace ComponentAutoBindTool.ViewCore
{
    public interface IViewManager
    {
        event Action<IWindowContext> OnViewOpened;
        event Action<IWindowContext> OnViewClosed;

        /// <summary>
        /// 强类型参数开窗主入口（推荐）
        /// </summary>
        int ShowView(string viewName, IViewArgs args = null, bool immediately = true);

        /// <summary>
        /// 兼容旧版 params object[] 开窗入口
        /// </summary>
        int ShowView(string viewName, bool immediately, params object[] args);

        /// <summary>
        /// 将窗口加入 Normal 等待队列，支持按 priority 优先级稳定插队（数值越大越优先出队，同优先级保持先进先出）
        /// </summary>
        void EnqueueView(string viewName, IViewArgs args = null, int priority = 0);

        /// <summary>
        /// 清空尚未出队的等待队列请求并归还对象池
        /// </summary>
        void ClearWaitingQueue();

        /// <summary>
        /// 事务安全地替换指定层级的栈顶窗口（解决 A-P07：新窗口准备成功后才关闭旧栈顶）
        /// </summary>
        int ReplaceTopView(string viewName, IViewArgs args = null, UILayer layer = UILayer.Normal);

        /// <summary>
        /// 按唯一 InstanceId 精确关闭窗口
        /// </summary>
        bool CloseView(int instanceId);

        /// <summary>
        /// 按窗口名关闭窗口（若有多个同名实例，优先关闭最顶层实例）
        /// </summary>
        bool CloseView(string viewName);

        /// <summary>
        /// 导航返回：按 System -> Top -> Normal 顺序关闭最顶层允许返回的窗口（解决 A-P01）
        /// </summary>
        bool TryNavigateBack();

        /// <summary>
        /// 关闭所有已打开窗口（可选是否连同 NonDestroy 缓存一并销毁）
        /// </summary>
        void CloseAllViews(bool destroyCached = false);

        bool IsViewOpen(string viewName);

        /// <summary>
        /// 异步等待指定窗口实例关闭（基于 Unity 6 原生 Awaitable，零第三方依赖；若窗口未打开或已关闭则同步立即返回）
        /// </summary>
        Awaitable WaitViewClosedAsync(int instanceId, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);
    }
}


==================== FILE: E:\Github Unity Projects\VContainer ComponentAutoBindTool\Assets\Third Party\ComponentAutoBindTool\Scripts\ViewCore\Contracts\IViewManagerDiagnostics.cs ====================

using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using UnityEngine;

namespace ComponentAutoBindTool.ViewCore
{
    public readonly struct WindowDiagnosticSnapshot
    {
        public readonly int InstanceId;
        public readonly string ViewName;
        public readonly UILayer Layer;
        public readonly WindowState State;
        public readonly WindowInstanceMode InstanceMode;
        public readonly ViewCoverMode CoverMode;
        public readonly ViewCloseStrategy CloseStrategy;
        public readonly int SortingOrder;
        public readonly int SubCanvasCount;
        public readonly float RemainingDestroyTime;
        public readonly bool IsTransitioning;
        public readonly bool AllowNavigateBack;
        public readonly GameObject HostObject;

        public WindowDiagnosticSnapshot(int instanceId, string viewName, UILayer layer, WindowState state,
            WindowInstanceMode instanceMode, ViewCoverMode coverMode, ViewCloseStrategy closeStrategy,
            int sortingOrder, int subCanvasCount, float remainingDestroyTime, bool isTransitioning,
            bool allowNavigateBack, GameObject hostObject)
        {
            InstanceId = instanceId;
            ViewName = viewName;
            Layer = layer;
            State = state;
            InstanceMode = instanceMode;
            CoverMode = coverMode;
            CloseStrategy = closeStrategy;
            SortingOrder = sortingOrder;
            SubCanvasCount = subCanvasCount;
            RemainingDestroyTime = remainingDestroyTime;
            IsTransitioning = isTransitioning;
            AllowNavigateBack = allowNavigateBack;
            HostObject = hostObject;
        }
    }

    public readonly struct QueuedRequestSnapshot
    {
        public readonly int QueueIndex;
        public readonly string ViewName;
        public readonly int Priority;
        public readonly string ArgsTypeName;

        public QueuedRequestSnapshot(int queueIndex, string viewName, int priority, string argsTypeName)
        {
            QueueIndex = queueIndex;
            ViewName = viewName;
            Priority = priority;
            ArgsTypeName = argsTypeName;
        }
    }

    public interface IViewManagerDiagnostics
    {
        bool IsDisposed { get; }
        int TotalInstanceCount { get; }
        int WaitingQueueCount { get; }

        // 实现方清空并复用调用方提供的缓冲区；层快照按栈底到栈顶排列。
        void GetLayerSnapshots(UILayer layer, List<WindowDiagnosticSnapshot> results);
        void GetInactiveSnapshots(List<WindowDiagnosticSnapshot> results);
        void GetWaitingQueueSnapshots(List<QueuedRequestSnapshot> results);
        bool CloseView(int instanceId);
        bool TryNavigateBack();
        void CloseAllViews(bool destroyCached = false);
        void ClearWaitingQueue();
    }

    public static class ViewManagerDebugRegistry
    {
        private static readonly List<IViewManagerDiagnostics> s_ActiveManagers = new();
        private static readonly ReadOnlyCollection<IViewManagerDiagnostics> s_ReadOnlyManagers =
            s_ActiveManagers.AsReadOnly();

        public static IReadOnlyList<IViewManagerDiagnostics> ActiveManagers
        {
            get
            {
                RemoveDisposedManagers();
                return s_ReadOnlyManagers;
            }
        }

        public static void Register(IViewManagerDiagnostics manager)
        {
            RemoveDisposedManagers();
            if (!IsAlive(manager))
            {
                return;
            }

            for (int i = 0; i < s_ActiveManagers.Count; i++)
            {
                if (ReferenceEquals(s_ActiveManagers[i], manager))
                {
                    return;
                }
            }

            s_ActiveManagers.Add(manager);
        }

        public static void Unregister(IViewManagerDiagnostics manager)
        {
            for (int i = s_ActiveManagers.Count - 1; i >= 0; i--)
            {
                if (ReferenceEquals(s_ActiveManagers[i], manager))
                {
                    s_ActiveManagers.RemoveAt(i);
                }
            }
        }

        [RuntimeInitializeOnLoadMethod(RuntimeInitializeLoadType.SubsystemRegistration)]
        private static void Reset()
        {
            s_ActiveManagers.Clear();
        }

        private static void RemoveDisposedManagers()
        {
            for (int i = s_ActiveManagers.Count - 1; i >= 0; i--)
            {
                if (!IsAlive(s_ActiveManagers[i]))
                {
                    s_ActiveManagers.RemoveAt(i);
                }
            }
        }

        private static bool IsAlive(IViewManagerDiagnostics manager)
        {
            return manager != null && !(manager is Object unityObject && unityObject == null) &&
                   !manager.IsDisposed;
        }
    }
}

using System;

// ReSharper disable once CheckNamespace
namespace ComponentAutoBindTool.ViewCore
{
    /// <summary>
    /// 窗口表现层入场/退场过渡动画契约。
    /// 业务层可通过在窗口 Prefab 上挂载实现此接口的 MonoBehaviour（如 Animator 或 DOTween 驱动器）接入动效。
    /// </summary>
    public interface IViewTransition
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// 播放入场过渡动画，完成后必须调用 onCompleted。
        /// </summary>
        void PlayEnter(ViewBase host, Action onCompleted);

        /// <summary>
        /// 播放退场过渡动画，完成后必须调用 onCompleted。
        /// </summary>
        void PlayExit(ViewBase host, Action onCompleted);

        /// <summary>
        /// 当窗口在过渡期间发生状态抢占（如退场中途被立即重开或强制销毁）时，立即中断当前动画。
        /// </summary>
        void Cancel(ViewBase host);
    }
}

==================== FILE: E:\Github Unity Projects\VContainer ComponentAutoBindTool\Assets\Third Party\ComponentAutoBindTool\Scripts\ViewCore\Contracts\ViewEnumsAndArgs.cs ====================

using System;
using UnityEngine;

// ReSharper disable once CheckNamespace
namespace ComponentAutoBindTool.ViewCore
{
    /// <summary>
    /// 窗口层级（各自拥有独立的顺序列表与排序区间）
    /// </summary>
    public enum UILayer
    {
        HUD = 0,
        Normal = 1,
        Top = 2,
        System = 3
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 窗口实例复用策略（解决 A-P02）
    /// </summary>
    public enum WindowInstanceMode
    {
        /// <summary>单实例：全局唯一，重复打开时从原位置抽离置顶并刷新参数（如背包、设置）</summary>
        Singleton = 0,
        /// <summary>多实例：每次打开分配新 InstanceId 并创建新实例（如独立详情弹窗）</summary>
        Multiple = 1
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 同层遮挡模式
    /// </summary>
    public enum ViewCoverMode
    {
        /// <summary>全屏/遮挡模式：暂停并隐藏同层位于其下方的窗口</summary>
        PausePrevious = 0,
        /// <summary>叠加模式：不隐藏同层位于其下方的窗口</summary>
        Overlay = 1
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 窗口关闭策略
    /// </summary>
    public enum ViewCloseStrategy
    {
        /// <summary>关闭后不销毁，保留子容器与 UIView 进入缓存池（自动清空 OpenSession 订阅）</summary>
        NonDestroy = 0,
        /// <summary>关闭后立即销毁</summary>
        DestroyImmediate = 1,
        /// <summary>关闭后延迟指定秒数销毁（期间若重开可拦截复活）</summary>
        DestroyDelayed = 2
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 窗口运行期精确状态（解决 A-P01 / A-P05）
    /// </summary>
    public enum WindowState
    {
        Constructing = 0,
        OpenAndActive = 1,
        Paused = 2,
        Cached = 3,
        PendingDestroy = 4,
        Destroyed = 5
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 强类型开窗参数标记接口（解决 A-P09）
    /// </summary>
    public interface IViewArgs { }

    /// <summary>
    /// 空参数单例
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class EmptyViewArgs : IViewArgs
    {
        public static readonly EmptyViewArgs Instance = new EmptyViewArgs();
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 兼容旧版 params object[] 的参数包装器
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class LegacyViewArgs : IViewArgs
    {
        public object[] DataBundle { get; }

        public LegacyViewArgs(params object[] dataBundle)
        {
            DataBundle = dataBundle ?? Array.Empty<object>();
        }

        public T Get<T>(int index, T defaultValue = default)
        {
            if (DataBundle != null && index >= 0 && index < DataBundle.Length && DataBundle[index] is T typedValue)
            {
                return typedValue;
            }
            return defaultValue;
        }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 框架独立日志出口（解决 A-P11，移除对项目 CCDebug 的硬编码依赖）
    /// </summary>
    public static class ViewLogger
    {
        public static Action<string> OnInfo = Debug.Log;
        public static Action<string> OnWarning = Debug.LogWarning;
        public static Action<string> OnError = Debug.LogError;

        public static void Info(string msg) => OnInfo?.Invoke($"[UIFramework] {msg}");
        public static void Warning(string msg) => OnWarning?.Invoke($"[UIFramework] {msg}");
        public static void Error(string msg) => OnError?.Invoke($"[UIFramework] {msg}");
    }
}

using System;

namespace ComponentAutoBindTool.ViewCore
{
    public static class ViewManagerTypedExtensions
    {
        public static int ShowView<TView, TArgs>(this IViewManager manager,
            ViewRoute<TView, TArgs> route, TArgs args, bool immediately = true)
            where TView : ViewBase where TArgs : class, IViewArgs =>
            RequireRoutes(manager).ShowView(route, args, immediately);

        public static int ShowView<TView>(this IViewManager manager,
            ViewRoute<TView, EmptyViewArgs> route, bool immediately = true)
            where TView : ViewBase => RequireRoutes(manager).ShowView(route, EmptyViewArgs.Instance, immediately);

        public static void EnqueueView<TView, TArgs>(this IViewManager manager,
            ViewRoute<TView, TArgs> route, TArgs args, int priority = 0)
            where TView : ViewBase where TArgs : class, IViewArgs =>
            RequireRoutes(manager).EnqueueView(route, args, priority);

        public static int ReplaceTopView<TView, TArgs>(this IViewManager manager,
            ViewRoute<TView, TArgs> route, TArgs args, UILayer layer = UILayer.Normal)
            where TView : ViewBase where TArgs : class, IViewArgs =>
            RequireRoutes(manager).ReplaceTopView(route, args, layer);

        public static bool CloseView<TView, TArgs>(this IViewManager manager, ViewRoute<TView, TArgs> route)
            where TView : ViewBase where TArgs : class, IViewArgs => RequireRoutes(manager).CloseView(route);

        private static IViewRouteManager RequireRoutes(IViewManager manager)
        {
            if (manager == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(manager));
            return manager as IViewRouteManager ?? throw new NotSupportedException(
                $"{manager.GetType().FullName} 未实现 IViewRouteManager，无法执行类型化路由。");
        }
    }
}


==================== FILE: E:\Github Unity Projects\VContainer ComponentAutoBindTool\Assets\Third Party\ComponentAutoBindTool\Scripts\ViewCore\Contracts\ViewRoute.cs ====================

using System;

namespace ComponentAutoBindTool.ViewCore
{
    /// <summary>不可变的资源键与宿主、参数契约。资源键由项目显式指定。</summary>
    public abstract class ViewRoute
    {
        public string Key { get; }
        public Type ViewType { get; }
        public Type ArgsType { get; }

        internal ViewRoute(string key, Type viewType, Type argsType)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(key)) throw new ArgumentException("窗口资源键不能为空。", nameof(key));
            Key = key;
            ViewType = viewType;
            ArgsType = argsType;
        }

        internal bool ValidateArgs(IViewArgs args)
        {
            if (args != null && ArgsType.IsInstanceOfType(args)) return true;
            ViewLogger.Error($"路由 [{Key}] 参数不匹配：预期 {ArgsType.FullName}，实际 {args?.GetType().FullName ?? "null"}。");
            return false;
        }

        internal bool ValidateHost(ViewBase host)
        {
            if (host != null && ViewType.IsInstanceOfType(host)) return true;
            ViewLogger.Error($"路由 [{Key}] 宿主不匹配：预期 {ViewType.FullName}，实际 {(host != null ? host.GetType().FullName : "null")}。");
            return false;
        }

        internal bool ValidatePresenter(IWindowPresenter presenter)
        {
            Type actual = (presenter as IWindowArgsContract)?.ArgsType;
            if (actual == ArgsType) return true;
            ViewLogger.Error($"路由 [{Key}] Presenter 参数契约不匹配：预期 {ArgsType.FullName}，实际 {actual?.FullName ?? "未提供 IWindowArgsContract"}，Presenter {presenter?.GetType().FullName ?? "null"}。");
            return false;
        }
    }

    public sealed class ViewRoute<TView, TArgs> : ViewRoute
        where TView : ViewBase where TArgs : class, IViewArgs
    {
        public ViewRoute(string key) : base(key, typeof(TView), typeof(TArgs)) { }
    }
}


==================== FILE: E:\Github Unity Projects\VContainer ComponentAutoBindTool\Assets\Third Party\ComponentAutoBindTool\Scripts\ViewCore\Management\ViewCloseWaiters.cs ====================

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Threading;
using UnityEngine;

namespace ComponentAutoBindTool.ViewCore
{
    /// <summary>
    /// 主线程拥有的关闭等待表。每次调用返回独立 Awaitable，调用方只能消费一次。
    /// </summary>
    internal sealed class ViewCloseWaiters : IDisposable
    {
        internal sealed class Batch
        {
            internal readonly ViewCloseWaiters Owner;
            internal readonly int InstanceId;
            internal readonly List<Waiter> Waiters = new();
            internal bool Detached;
            internal bool Completed;

            internal Batch(ViewCloseWaiters owner, int instanceId)
            {
                Owner = owner;
                InstanceId = instanceId;
            }
        }

        // 节点不复用：旧批次和已派发的取消回调始终指向原租约，不能误完成复用后的源。
        internal sealed class Waiter
        {
            internal readonly Batch Batch;
            internal AwaitableCompletionSource Source;
            internal CancellationTokenRegistration Registration;
            internal bool Completed;

            internal Waiter(Batch batch, AwaitableCompletionSource source)
            {
                Batch = batch;
                Source = source;
            }
        }

        private readonly int m_MainThreadId = Thread.CurrentThread.ManagedThreadId;
        private readonly Dictionary<int, Batch> m_ActiveBatches = new();
        private readonly HashSet<Batch> m_DetachedBatches = new();
        private readonly Stack<AwaitableCompletionSource> m_SourcePool = new();
        private bool m_IsDisposed;

        internal async Awaitable Wait(int instanceId, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
        {
            EnsureMainThread();
            if (m_IsDisposed) return;
            cancellationToken.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();

            if (!m_ActiveBatches.TryGetValue(instanceId, out Batch batch))
            {
                batch = new Batch(this, instanceId);
                m_ActiveBatches.Add(instanceId, batch);
            }

            // 新建源已准备好；池中源只在上一次 await 消费后 Reset 一次。
            AwaitableCompletionSource source = m_SourcePool.Count > 0
                ? m_SourcePool.Pop()
                : new AwaitableCompletionSource();
            var waiter = new Waiter(batch, source);
            batch.Waiters.Add(waiter);

            if (cancellationToken.CanBeCanceled)
            {
                waiter.Registration = cancellationToken.Register(static state =>
                {
                    var pending = (Waiter)state;
                    pending.Batch.Owner.RequestCancellation(pending);
                }, waiter);
            }

            try
            {
                await source.Awaitable;
            }
            finally
            {
                // 所有完成均在主线程；外层返回值可以晚于此处才被调用方消费。
                // 注销并等待正在执行的取消注册，随后才允许复用完成源。
                waiter.Registration.Dispose();
                waiter.Source = null;
                if (!m_IsDisposed)
                {
                    source.Reset();
                    m_SourcePool.Push(source);
                }
            }
        }

        internal Batch Detach(int instanceId)
        {
            EnsureMainThread();
            if (!m_ActiveBatches.Remove(instanceId, out Batch batch)) return null;
            batch.Detached = true;
            m_DetachedBatches.Add(batch);
            return batch;
        }

        internal void Complete(Batch batch)
        {
            EnsureMainThread();
            if (batch == null) return;
            if (!ReferenceEquals(batch.Owner, this) || !batch.Detached)
                throw new ArgumentException("只能完成由当前等待表分离的批次。", nameof(batch));
            if (batch.Completed) return;

            // 先提交批次终态，再调用可能同步重入管理器的 continuation。
            batch.Completed = true;
            m_DetachedBatches.Remove(batch);
            for (int index = 0; index < batch.Waiters.Count; index++)
            {
                CompleteWaiter(batch.Waiters[index], false);
            }
            batch.Waiters.Clear();
        }

        public void Dispose()
        {
            EnsureMainThread();
            if (m_IsDisposed) return;
            m_IsDisposed = true;

            // 首先分离全部批次，后续 continuation 不会修改正在遍历的集合。
            foreach (Batch batch in m_ActiveBatches.Values)
            {
                batch.Detached = true;
                m_DetachedBatches.Add(batch);
            }
            m_ActiveBatches.Clear();
            var pendingBatches = new List<Batch>(m_DetachedBatches);
            m_DetachedBatches.Clear();
            m_SourcePool.Clear();
            foreach (Batch batch in pendingBatches)
            {
                Complete(batch);
            }
        }

        private void RequestCancellation(Waiter waiter)
        {
            if (Thread.CurrentThread.ManagedThreadId == m_MainThreadId)
            {
                CompleteWaiter(waiter, true);
            }
            else
            {
                CancelOnMainThread(waiter);
            }
        }

        // CancellationToken 可以从后台线程触发；回调不在后台访问表、节点状态或对象池。
        private async void CancelOnMainThread(Waiter waiter)
        {
            await Awaitable.MainThreadAsync();
            CompleteWaiter(waiter, true);
        }

        private void CompleteWaiter(Waiter waiter, bool canceled)
        {
            EnsureMainThread();
            if (waiter.Completed) return;
            waiter.Completed = true;

            Batch batch = waiter.Batch;
            if (!batch.Detached)
            {
                batch.Waiters.Remove(waiter);
                if (batch.Waiters.Count == 0)
                {
                    m_ActiveBatches.Remove(batch.InstanceId);
                }
            }

            // Detach 后保留节点到批次处理结束；已取消节点会被 Complete 跳过。
            if (canceled) waiter.Source.TrySetCanceled();
            else waiter.Source.TrySetResult();
        }

        private void EnsureMainThread()
        {
            if (Thread.CurrentThread.ManagedThreadId != m_MainThreadId)
                throw new InvalidOperationException("关闭等待表只能从创建它的 Unity 主线程访问。");
        }
    }
}


==================== FILE: E:\Github Unity Projects\VContainer ComponentAutoBindTool\Assets\Third Party\ComponentAutoBindTool\Scripts\ViewCore\Management\ViewManager.cs ====================

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Threading;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.Pool;
using VContainer;
using VContainer.Unity;

// ReSharper disable once CheckNamespace
namespace ComponentAutoBindTool.ViewCore
{
