    public sealed class ViewManager : IViewManager, IViewManagerDiagnostics, IViewRouteManager, ITickable, IDisposable
    {
        private const int SortingStepPerWindow = 50;
        private const int MaxWindowsPerLayer = 38; // 50 * 38 = 1900，确保不跨层越界（解决 A-P08）

        private readonly IObjectResolver m_ParentResolver;
        private readonly IViewPrefabHelper m_PrefabHelper;
        private readonly IViewRootHelper m_RootHelper;

        private int m_NextInstanceId = 1;
        private bool m_IsDisposed;
        private readonly ViewCloseWaiters m_CloseWaiters = new();
        private readonly Dictionary<UILayer, int> m_PresentationVersions = new();

        public bool BlockQueueWhenHigherLayersOpen { get; set; } = true;
        public bool IsDisposed => m_IsDisposed;
        public int TotalInstanceCount => m_AllEntries.Count;
        public int WaitingQueueCount => m_WaitingQueue.Count;

        // 1. 全局唯一实例登记表：InstanceId -> WindowEntry（覆盖打开、暂停、缓存、延迟销毁所有状态，解决 A-P01）
        private readonly Dictionary<int, WindowEntry> m_AllEntries = new();

        // 2. 单实例索引：ViewName -> InstanceId（解决 A-P02）
        private readonly Dictionary<string, int> m_SingletonIndex = new();

        // 3. 四层独立可重排顺序列表：保存当前处于打开/暂停栈中的 InstanceId（解决 A-P01 / A-P02）
        private readonly Dictionary<UILayer, List<int>> m_LayerOrderLists = new()
        {
            { UILayer.HUD, new List<int>() },
            { UILayer.Normal, new List<int>() },
            { UILayer.Top, new List<int>() },
            { UILayer.System, new List<int>() }
        };

        // 4. 延迟销毁真实时间倒计时表：InstanceId -> 剩余秒数（脱离 ParentScope 协程，支持重开拦截复活与统一 Dispose，解决 A-P06）
        private readonly Dictionary<int, float> m_PendingDestroyTimers = new();
        private readonly List<int> m_ExpiredDestroyIdsCache = new();
        private readonly List<int> m_TickIdsCache = new();
        private readonly List<int> m_TimerIdsCache = new();

        // 5. 排队延后打开队列
        private sealed class QueuedOpenRequest
        {
            public string ViewName;
            public IViewArgs Args;
            public int Priority;
            public string ArgsTypeName;
            public ViewRoute Route;
            public void Reset() { ViewName = null; Args = null; Priority = 0; ArgsTypeName = null; Route = null; }
        }
        private readonly List<QueuedOpenRequest> m_WaitingQueue = new();

        public event Action<IWindowContext> OnViewOpened;
        public event Action<IWindowContext> OnViewClosed;

        [Preserve]
        public ViewManager(
            IObjectResolver parentResolver,
            IViewPrefabHelper prefabHelper,
            IViewRootHelper rootHelper)
        {
            m_ParentResolver = parentResolver;
            m_PrefabHelper = prefabHelper;
            m_RootHelper = rootHelper;
            ViewManagerDebugRegistry.Register(this);
        }

        int IViewRouteManager.ShowView(ViewRoute route, IViewArgs args, bool immediately)
        {
            if (m_IsDisposed || !ValidateRouteInput(route, args)) return 0;
            if (!immediately)
            {
                EnqueueInternal(route.Key, args, 0, route);
                return 0;
            }
            return ShowViewImmediateInternal(route.Key, args, route);
        }

        void IViewRouteManager.EnqueueView(ViewRoute route, IViewArgs args, int priority)
        {
            if (m_IsDisposed || !ValidateRouteInput(route, args)) return;
            EnqueueInternal(route.Key, args, priority, route);
        }

        int IViewRouteManager.ReplaceTopView(ViewRoute route, IViewArgs args, UILayer layer)
        {
            if (m_IsDisposed || !ValidateRouteInput(route, args)) return 0;
            return ReplaceTopViewInternal(route.Key, args, layer, route);
        }

        bool IViewRouteManager.CloseView(ViewRoute route)
        {
            if (m_IsDisposed || !ValidateRoute(route)) return false;
            var entry = FindEntryToClose(route.Key);
            return entry != null && route.ValidateHost(entry.ViewHost) &&
                   route.ValidatePresenter(entry.Presenter) && CloseView(entry.InstanceId);
        }

        private static bool ValidateRouteInput(ViewRoute route, IViewArgs args) =>
            ValidateRoute(route) && route.ValidateArgs(args);

        private static bool ValidateRoute(ViewRoute route)
        {
            if (route != null) return true;
            ViewLogger.Error("窗口路由不能为空。");
            return false;
        }

        public int ShowView(string viewName, bool immediately, params object[] args)
        {
            IViewArgs wrappedArgs = (args == null || args.Length == 0)
                ? EmptyViewArgs.Instance
                : new LegacyViewArgs(args);
            return ShowView(viewName, wrappedArgs, immediately);
        }

        public int ShowView(string viewName, IViewArgs args = null, bool immediately = true)
        {
            if (m_IsDisposed || string.IsNullOrEmpty(viewName)) return 0;

            if (!immediately)
            {
                EnqueueView(viewName, args);
                return 0;
            }

            return ShowViewImmediateInternal(viewName, args ?? EmptyViewArgs.Instance);
        }

        public void EnqueueView(string viewName, IViewArgs args = null, int priority = 0)
        {
            if (m_IsDisposed || string.IsNullOrEmpty(viewName)) return;
            EnqueueInternal(viewName, args ?? EmptyViewArgs.Instance, priority, null);
        }

        private void EnqueueInternal(string viewName, IViewArgs args, int priority, ViewRoute route)
        {
            var request = GenericPool<QueuedOpenRequest>.Get();
            request.ViewName = viewName;
            request.Args = args ?? EmptyViewArgs.Instance;
            request.ArgsTypeName = request.Args.GetType().Name;
            request.Priority = priority;
            request.Route = route;
            int index = 0;
            while (index < m_WaitingQueue.Count && m_WaitingQueue[index].Priority >= priority) index++;
            m_WaitingQueue.Insert(index, request);
        }

        public void ClearWaitingQueue()
        {
            foreach (var request in m_WaitingQueue)
            {
                request.Reset();
                GenericPool<QueuedOpenRequest>.Release(request);
            }
            m_WaitingQueue.Clear();
        }

        public async Awaitable WaitViewClosedAsync(int instanceId, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
        {
            if (m_IsDisposed || !m_AllEntries.TryGetValue(instanceId, out var entry) ||
                (!entry.HasOpenSession && entry.State != WindowState.OpenAndActive && entry.State != WindowState.Paused)) return;
            await m_CloseWaiters.Wait(instanceId, cancellationToken);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 事务安全地替换指定层栈顶窗口（解决 A-P07：先确认新窗口准备就绪，再关闭原栈顶）
        /// </summary>
        public int ReplaceTopView(string viewName, IViewArgs args = null, UILayer layer = UILayer.Normal)
        {
            if (m_IsDisposed || string.IsNullOrEmpty(viewName)) return 0;
            return ReplaceTopViewInternal(viewName, args ?? EmptyViewArgs.Instance, layer, null);
        }

        private int ReplaceTopViewInternal(string viewName, IViewArgs args, UILayer layer, ViewRoute route)
        {

            var layerList = m_LayerOrderLists[layer];
            int oldTopInstanceId = layerList.Count > 0 ? layerList[^1] : 0;

            // 先准备或获取目标窗口实例
            WindowEntry targetEntry = GetOrPrepareWindowEntry(viewName, route);
            if (targetEntry == null)
            {
                ViewLogger.Error($"ReplaceTopView 失败：新窗口 [{viewName}] 准备失败，保留原栈顶窗口不关闭。");
                return 0;
            }

            // 如果新窗口就是当前栈顶自身，直接刷新参数即可
            if (oldTopInstanceId != 0 && oldTopInstanceId != targetEntry.InstanceId)
            {
                CloseView(oldTopInstanceId);
            }

            return ActivateAndScheduleEntry(targetEntry, args ?? EmptyViewArgs.Instance) ? targetEntry.InstanceId : 0;
        }

        private int ShowViewImmediateInternal(string viewName, IViewArgs args, ViewRoute route = null)
        {
            WindowEntry entry = GetOrPrepareWindowEntry(viewName, route);
            if (entry == null) return 0;

            return ActivateAndScheduleEntry(entry, args) ? entry.InstanceId : 0;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 获取已有可复用 Singleton 实例（含打开中、暂停中、NonDestroy 缓存中、延迟销毁等待中），或创建新实例
        /// </summary>
        private WindowEntry GetOrPrepareWindowEntry(string viewName, ViewRoute route = null)
        {
            // 1. 检查是否命中已有 Singleton 实例（彻底解决 A-P02 的 A->B->A 重复创建问题）
            if (m_SingletonIndex.TryGetValue(viewName, out int existingId))
            {
                if (m_AllEntries.TryGetValue(existingId, out var existingEntry) &&
                    existingEntry.GameObject != null &&
                    existingEntry.State != WindowState.Destroyed)
                {
                    // 当前 Close 回调尚未返回时，不能在同一个 Presenter 上交叠开启新会话。
                    if (existingEntry.IsClosing) return null;
                    if (route != null && (!route.ValidateHost(existingEntry.ViewHost) ||
                                          !route.ValidatePresenter(existingEntry.Presenter))) return null;
                    // 若该实例正在延迟销毁倒计时中，直接拦截并取消销毁任务！
                    m_PendingDestroyTimers.Remove(existingId);
                    return existingEntry;
                }

                // 若记录已失效，清理残余索引
                m_SingletonIndex.Remove(viewName);
                if (existingEntry != null) FinalizeDestroyEntry(existingEntry);
            }

            // 2. 创建全新实例（四阶段流水线的前三阶段：准备 Prefab -> 实例化宿主 -> 显式构建子容器与 OnCreate）
            GameObject prefab = m_PrefabHelper.GetViewPrefab(viewName);
            if (prefab == null)
            {
                string contract = route == null ? "" : $" 预期宿主 {route.ViewType.FullName}，实际 null。";
                ViewLogger.Error($"无法打开窗口 [{viewName}]：IViewPrefabHelper 返回 Prefab 为 null。" + contract);
                return null;
            }

            var prefabViewComponent = prefab.GetComponent<ViewBase>();
            if (route != null && !route.ValidateHost(prefabViewComponent)) return null;
            UILayer targetLayer = prefabViewComponent != null ? prefabViewComponent.Layer : UILayer.Normal;
            Transform root = m_RootHelper.GetViewRoot(viewName, targetLayer);

            GameObject instanceGo = UnityEngine.Object.Instantiate(prefab, root);
            if (!instanceGo.TryGetComponent<ViewBase>(out var viewHost))
            {
                ViewLogger.Error($"窗口 Prefab [{viewName}] 根节点缺少继承自 ViewBase 的脚本！");
                m_PrefabHelper.ReleaseViewInstance(viewName, instanceGo);
                return null;
            }

            int newId = m_NextInstanceId++;
            var newEntry = new WindowEntry(newId, viewName, viewHost, this);

            bool prepared;
            try
            {
                prepared = newEntry.BuildScopeAndCreate(m_ParentResolver, route);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                ViewLogger.Error($"窗口 [{viewName}] 构建子容器或执行 OnCreate 异常: {ex}");
                try { newEntry.Dispose(); }
                finally { m_PrefabHelper.ReleaseViewInstance(viewName, instanceGo); }
                return null;
            }

            if (!prepared || m_IsDisposed)
            {
                try { newEntry.Dispose(); }
                finally { m_PrefabHelper.ReleaseViewInstance(viewName, instanceGo); }
                return null;
            }

            m_AllEntries[newId] = newEntry;
            if (viewHost.InstanceMode == WindowInstanceMode.Singleton)
            {
                m_SingletonIndex[viewName] = newId;
            }

            return newEntry;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 第四阶段：将准备好的窗口移至对应层列表顶部、重算遮挡排序、交付参数并触发恢复/打开事件
        /// </summary>
        private bool ActivateAndScheduleEntry(WindowEntry entry, IViewArgs args)
        {
            if (m_IsDisposed || !m_AllEntries.ContainsKey(entry.InstanceId) || entry.GameObject == null) return false;
            var layerList = m_LayerOrderLists[entry.Layer];

            // 若已在列表中（例如 A->B->A 或栈顶刷新），先移除原位置再追加到末尾置顶
            layerList.Remove(entry.InstanceId);
            layerList.Add(entry.InstanceId);

            // 每次开窗必交付新参数（触发 Presenter.OnOpen 与子控件级联 OnOpen，解决 A-P04）
            entry.DeliverOpenArgs(args);

            if (m_IsDisposed || !m_AllEntries.ContainsKey(entry.InstanceId) || !layerList.Contains(entry.InstanceId)) return false;

            // 重新计算该层的 SortingOrder 与遮挡暂停/恢复状态
            RefreshLayerPresentation(entry.Layer);

            if (m_IsDisposed || !m_AllEntries.ContainsKey(entry.InstanceId) || !layerList.Contains(entry.InstanceId)) return false;
            OnViewOpened?.Invoke(entry);
            return true;
        }

        public bool CloseView(int instanceId)
        {
            if (m_IsDisposed || !m_AllEntries.TryGetValue(instanceId, out var entry))
                return false;

            // 若已经处于关闭缓存、待销毁或已销毁状态，无需重复关闭
            if ((!entry.HasOpenSession && (entry.State == WindowState.Cached ||
                entry.State == WindowState.PendingDestroy)) ||
                entry.State == WindowState.Destroyed)
            {
                return false;
            }

            UILayer layer = entry.Layer;
            m_LayerOrderLists[layer].Remove(instanceId);

            // 完成源在用户回调前与当前会话分离；回调重开后的等待属于新会话。
            var closedWaiters = m_CloseWaiters.Detach(instanceId);
            List<Exception> errors = null;
            var strategy = entry.ViewHost.CloseStrategy;
            float delay = entry.ViewHost.DestroyDelaySeconds;
            try
            {
                switch (strategy)
                {
                    case ViewCloseStrategy.NonDestroy:
                        entry.EnterClosedState(WindowState.Cached);
                        break;
                    case ViewCloseStrategy.DestroyDelayed when delay > 0f:
                        m_PendingDestroyTimers[instanceId] = delay;
                        entry.EnterClosedState(WindowState.PendingDestroy, () =>
                        {
                            if (m_PendingDestroyTimers.TryGetValue(instanceId, out float remaining) && remaining <= 0f)
                                FinalizeDestroyEntry(entry);
                        });
                        break;
                    default:
                        if (m_SingletonIndex.TryGetValue(entry.ViewName, out int mappedId) && mappedId == instanceId)
                            m_SingletonIndex.Remove(entry.ViewName);
                        entry.EnterClosedState(WindowState.Destroyed, () => FinalizeDestroyEntry(entry));
                        break;
                }
            }
            catch (Exception error) { ViewLifecycleErrors.Add(ref errors, error); }
            try { RefreshLayerPresentation(layer); }
            catch (Exception error) { ViewLifecycleErrors.Add(ref errors, error); }
            try { OnViewClosed?.Invoke(entry); }
            catch (Exception error) { ViewLifecycleErrors.Add(ref errors, error); }
            finally { m_CloseWaiters.Complete(closedWaiters); }
            ViewLifecycleErrors.Throw(errors);
            return true;
        }

        public bool CloseView(string viewName)
        {
            var entry = FindEntryToClose(viewName);
            return entry != null && CloseView(entry.InstanceId);
        }

        private WindowEntry FindEntryToClose(string viewName)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(viewName)) return null;

            if (m_SingletonIndex.TryGetValue(viewName, out int singleId))
            {
                return m_AllEntries.GetValueOrDefault(singleId);
            }

            // 若是多实例窗口，从高到低查找最后打开的同名实例并关闭
            UILayer[] searchOrder = { UILayer.System, UILayer.Top, UILayer.Normal, UILayer.HUD };
            foreach (var layer in searchOrder)
            {
                var list = m_LayerOrderLists[layer];
                for (int i = list.Count - 1; i >= 0; i--)
                {
                    var entry = m_AllEntries[list[i]];
                    if (entry.ViewName == viewName)
                    {
                        return entry;
                    }
                }
            }
            return null;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 导航返回：按 System -> Top -> Normal 从高到低寻找顶层可返回窗口（解决 A-P01）
        /// </summary>
        public bool TryNavigateBack()
        {
            UILayer[] backLayers = { UILayer.System, UILayer.Top, UILayer.Normal };
            for (int l = 0; l < backLayers.Length; l++)
            {
                var list = m_LayerOrderLists[backLayers[l]];
                for (int i = list.Count - 1; i >= 0; i--)
                {
                    var entry = m_AllEntries[list[i]];
                    if (entry.ViewHost.AllowNavigateBack)
                    {
                        return CloseView(entry.InstanceId);
                    }
                }
            }
            return false;
        }

        public void CloseAllViews(bool destroyCached = false)
        {
            if (m_IsDisposed) return;
            var ids = ListPool<int>.Get();
            List<Exception> errors = null;
            try
            {
                foreach (var layer in new[] { UILayer.System, UILayer.Top, UILayer.Normal, UILayer.HUD })
                {
                    var list = m_LayerOrderLists[layer];
                    for (int i = list.Count - 1; i >= 0; i--) ids.Add(list[i]);
                }
                foreach (int id in ids)
                {
                    try { CloseView(id); }
                    catch (Exception error) { ViewLifecycleErrors.Add(ref errors, error); }
                }
                if (destroyCached)
                {
                    ids.Clear();
                    ids.AddRange(m_AllEntries.Keys);
                    foreach (int id in ids)
                    {
                        if (!m_AllEntries.TryGetValue(id, out var entry) ||
                            entry.State == WindowState.OpenAndActive || entry.State == WindowState.Paused) continue;
                        try { FinalizeDestroyEntry(entry); }
                        catch (Exception error) { ViewLifecycleErrors.Add(ref errors, error); }
                    }
                }
            }
            finally { ListPool<int>.Release(ids); }
            ViewLifecycleErrors.Throw(errors);
        }

        public bool IsViewOpen(string viewName)
        {
            foreach (var kvp in m_AllEntries)
            {
                var entry = kvp.Value;
                if (entry.ViewName == viewName &&
                    (entry.HasOpenSession || entry.State == WindowState.OpenAndActive || entry.State == WindowState.Paused))
                {
                    return true;
                }
            }
            return false;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 重新计算指定层级的 Canvas 排序与全屏遮挡状态（借鉴对象 B 的同层遮挡扫描）
        /// </summary>
        private void RefreshLayerPresentation(UILayer layer)
        {
            m_PresentationVersions.TryGetValue(layer, out int version);
            m_PresentationVersions[layer] = ++version;
            var liveList = m_LayerOrderLists[layer];
            if (liveList.Count == 0) return;
            var list = ListPool<int>.Get();
            list.AddRange(liveList);
            List<Exception> errors = null;
            try
            {

                int baseOrder = GetLayerBaseOrder(layer);
                if (list.Count > MaxWindowsPerLayer)
                {
                    ViewLogger.Warning($"层级 [{layer}] 当前打开窗口数 ({list.Count}) 超过预算上限 ({MaxWindowsPerLayer})，请注意排序区间！");
                }

                // 1. 自底向上设置根 Canvas 及子 Canvas 的相对排序
                for (int i = 0; i < list.Count; i++)
                {
                    if (m_AllEntries.TryGetValue(list[i], out var entry))
                    {
                        int clampedIndex = Mathf.Min(i, MaxWindowsPerLayer);
                        entry.SetSortingOrder(baseOrder + clampedIndex * SortingStepPerWindow);
                    }
                }

                // 2. 自顶向下扫描同层遮挡：遇到第一个 PausePrevious 窗口后，其下方所有同层窗口标记为遮挡暂停
                int firstFullScreenIndex = -1;
                for (int i = list.Count - 1; i >= 0; i--)
                {
                    if (!m_AllEntries.TryGetValue(list[i], out var entry)) continue;
                    if (entry.ViewHost.CoverMode == ViewCoverMode.PausePrevious)
                    {
                        firstFullScreenIndex = i;
                        break;
                    }
                }

                // 先将被遮挡的下层窗口 Pause，再将可见的上层窗口 Resume
                for (int i = 0; i < firstFullScreenIndex; i++)
                {
                    if (m_IsDisposed || m_PresentationVersions[layer] != version) break;
                    if (!m_AllEntries.TryGetValue(list[i], out var entry)) continue;
                    try { entry.Pause(); }
                    catch (Exception error) { ViewLifecycleErrors.Add(ref errors, error); }
                }
                for (int i = Math.Max(0, firstFullScreenIndex); i < list.Count; i++)
                {
                    if (m_IsDisposed || m_PresentationVersions[layer] != version) break;
                    if (!m_AllEntries.TryGetValue(list[i], out var entry)) continue;
                    try { entry.SetActiveAndResume(); }
                    catch (Exception error) { ViewLifecycleErrors.Add(ref errors, error); }
                }
            }
            finally { ListPool<int>.Release(list); }
            ViewLifecycleErrors.Throw(errors);
        }

        private static int GetLayerBaseOrder(UILayer layer)
        {
            return layer switch
            {
                UILayer.HUD => 1000,
                UILayer.Normal => 2000,
                UILayer.Top => 4000,
                UILayer.System => 6000,
                _ => 2000
            };
        }

        public void Tick()
        {
            if (m_IsDisposed) return;

            // 1. 当 Normal 层为空时，处理排队请求
            if (m_LayerOrderLists[UILayer.Normal].Count == 0 && m_WaitingQueue.Count > 0 &&
                (!BlockQueueWhenHigherLayersOpen ||
                 (m_LayerOrderLists[UILayer.Top].Count == 0 && m_LayerOrderLists[UILayer.System].Count == 0)))
            {
                var req = m_WaitingQueue[0];
                m_WaitingQueue.RemoveAt(0);
                try
                {
                    ShowViewImmediateInternal(req.ViewName, req.Args, req.Route);
                }
                finally
                {
                    req.Reset();
                    GenericPool<QueuedOpenRequest>.Release(req);
                }
            }

            // 2. 驱动所有处于 OpenAndActive 窗口及其可见子 Widget 的受控帧更新（解决 A-P05）
            float dt = Time.deltaTime;
            m_TickIdsCache.Clear();
            foreach (var id in m_AllEntries.Keys)
            {
                m_TickIdsCache.Add(id);
            }
            for (int i = 0; i < m_TickIdsCache.Count; i++)
            {
                if (m_AllEntries.TryGetValue(m_TickIdsCache[i], out var entry) &&
                    entry.State == WindowState.OpenAndActive)
                {
                    entry.Tick(dt);
                }
            }

            // 3. 驱动真实时间延迟销毁倒计时（解决 A-P06）
            if (m_PendingDestroyTimers.Count > 0)
            {
                float unscaledDt = Time.unscaledDeltaTime;
                m_ExpiredDestroyIdsCache.Clear();

                m_TimerIdsCache.Clear();
                m_TimerIdsCache.AddRange(m_PendingDestroyTimers.Keys);
                foreach (int timerId in m_TimerIdsCache)
                {
                    if (!m_PendingDestroyTimers.TryGetValue(timerId, out float remaining)) continue;
                    remaining = Mathf.Max(0f, remaining - unscaledDt);
                    m_PendingDestroyTimers[timerId] = remaining;
                    if (remaining <= 0f && m_AllEntries.TryGetValue(timerId, out var pending) && !pending.IsTransitioning)
                    {
                        m_ExpiredDestroyIdsCache.Add(timerId);
                    }
                }

                for (int i = 0; i < m_ExpiredDestroyIdsCache.Count; i++)
                {
                    int expiredId = m_ExpiredDestroyIdsCache[i];
                    if (m_PendingDestroyTimers.TryGetValue(expiredId, out float remaining) && remaining <= 0f &&
                        m_AllEntries.TryGetValue(expiredId, out var expiredEntry) &&
                        expiredEntry.State == WindowState.PendingDestroy && !expiredEntry.IsTransitioning)
                    {
                        FinalizeDestroyEntry(expiredEntry);
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        private void FinalizeDestroyEntry(WindowEntry entry)
        {
            int id = entry.InstanceId;
            string viewName = entry.ViewName;
            GameObject go = entry.GameObject;

            if (!m_AllEntries.Remove(id)) return;
            m_PendingDestroyTimers.Remove(id);
            if (m_SingletonIndex.TryGetValue(viewName, out int mappedId) && mappedId == id)
            {
                m_SingletonIndex.Remove(viewName);
            }

            var waiters = m_CloseWaiters.Detach(id);
            List<Exception> errors = null;
            try { entry.Dispose(); }
            catch (Exception error) { ViewLifecycleErrors.Add(ref errors, error); }
            try
            {
                if (go != null) m_PrefabHelper.ReleaseViewInstance(viewName, go);
            }
            catch (Exception error) { ViewLifecycleErrors.Add(ref errors, error); }
            finally { m_CloseWaiters.Complete(waiters); }
            ViewLifecycleErrors.Throw(errors);
        }

        public void GetLayerSnapshots(UILayer layer, List<WindowDiagnosticSnapshot> results)
        {
            if (results == null) return;
            results.Clear();
            if (!m_LayerOrderLists.TryGetValue(layer, out var ids)) return;
            foreach (int id in ids)
                if (m_AllEntries.TryGetValue(id, out var entry)) results.Add(BuildSnapshot(entry));
        }

        public void GetInactiveSnapshots(List<WindowDiagnosticSnapshot> results)
        {
            if (results == null) return;
            results.Clear();
            foreach (var entry in m_AllEntries.Values)
                if (entry.State == WindowState.Cached || entry.State == WindowState.PendingDestroy ||
                    (entry.State == WindowState.Destroyed && entry.IsTransitioning)) results.Add(BuildSnapshot(entry));
        }

        public void GetWaitingQueueSnapshots(List<QueuedRequestSnapshot> results)
        {
            if (results == null) return;
            results.Clear();
            for (int i = 0; i < m_WaitingQueue.Count; i++)
            {
                var request = m_WaitingQueue[i];
                results.Add(new QueuedRequestSnapshot(i, request.ViewName, request.Priority, request.ArgsTypeName));
            }
        }

        private WindowDiagnosticSnapshot BuildSnapshot(WindowEntry entry)
        {
            m_PendingDestroyTimers.TryGetValue(entry.InstanceId, out float remaining);
            var host = entry.ViewHost;
            return new WindowDiagnosticSnapshot(entry.InstanceId, entry.ViewName, entry.Layer, entry.State,
                host != null ? host.InstanceMode : WindowInstanceMode.Singleton,
                host != null ? host.CoverMode : ViewCoverMode.Overlay,
                host != null ? host.CloseStrategy : ViewCloseStrategy.NonDestroy,
                entry.RootSortingOrder, entry.SubCanvasCount, remaining, entry.IsTransitioning,
                host != null && host.AllowNavigateBack, entry.GameObject);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 管理器整体幂等释放入口（随父容器 Dispose 自动调用，彻底解决 A-P06）
        /// </summary>
        public void Dispose()
        {
            if (m_IsDisposed) return;
            m_IsDisposed = true;
            ViewManagerDebugRegistry.Unregister(this);
            ClearWaitingQueue();

            m_PendingDestroyTimers.Clear();
            foreach (var list in m_LayerOrderLists.Values)
            {
                list.Clear();
            }
            m_SingletonIndex.Clear();

            // 先切断登记表，销毁回调和等待 continuation 可以安全重入查询。
            var entries = ListPool<WindowEntry>.Get();
            entries.AddRange(m_AllEntries.Values);
            m_AllEntries.Clear();
            List<Exception> errors = null;
            foreach (var entry in entries)
            {
                GameObject go = entry.GameObject;
                string viewName = entry.ViewName;
                try { entry.Dispose(); }
                catch (Exception error) { ViewLifecycleErrors.Add(ref errors, error); }
                try
                {
                    if (go != null) m_PrefabHelper.ReleaseViewInstance(viewName, go);
                }
                catch (Exception error) { ViewLifecycleErrors.Add(ref errors, error); }
            }
            ListPool<WindowEntry>.Release(entries);
            m_CloseWaiters.Dispose();
            ViewLifecycleErrors.Throw(errors);
        }
    }
}


==================== FILE: E:\Github Unity Projects\VContainer ComponentAutoBindTool\Assets\Third Party\ComponentAutoBindTool\Scripts\ViewCore\Management\WindowEntry.cs ====================

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Runtime.ExceptionServices;
using System.Threading;
using UnityEngine;
using VContainer;

namespace ComponentAutoBindTool.ViewCore
{
    public sealed class WindowEntry : IWindowContext, IDisposable
    {
        public int InstanceId { get; }
        public string ViewName { get; }
        public ViewBase ViewHost { get; }
        public GameObject GameObject => ViewHost != null ? ViewHost.gameObject : null;
        public UILayer Layer { get; }
        public WindowState State { get; private set; } = WindowState.Constructing;
        public bool IsTransitioning { get; private set; }
        public int RootSortingOrder => m_RootCanvas != null ? m_RootCanvas.sortingOrder : 0;
        public int SubCanvasCount => m_SubCanvases.Count;

        public IWindowPresenter Presenter { get; private set; }

        private readonly IViewManager m_ViewManager;
        private readonly Canvas m_RootCanvas;
        private readonly List<(Canvas canvas, int relativeOffset)> m_SubCanvases = new();
        private IScopedObjectResolver m_ChildScope;
        private bool m_IsDisposed;
        private int m_TransitionVersion;
        internal bool IsClosing { get; private set; }
        internal bool HasOpenSession { get; private set; }

        public WindowEntry(int instanceId, string viewName, ViewBase viewHost, IViewManager viewManager)
        {
            InstanceId = instanceId;
            ViewName = viewName;
            ViewHost = viewHost;
            Layer = viewHost.Layer;
            m_ViewManager = viewManager;

            m_RootCanvas = viewHost.GetComponent<Canvas>();
            if (m_RootCanvas != null)
            {
                m_RootCanvas.overrideSorting = true;
                int rootBaseOrder = m_RootCanvas.sortingOrder;

                // 收集所有子 Canvas 相对于根 Canvas 的初始排序偏移（解决 A-P08）
                var allCanvases = viewHost.GetComponentsInChildren<Canvas>(true);
                for (int i = 0; i < allCanvases.Length; i++)
                {
                    var c = allCanvases[i];
                    if (c != m_RootCanvas)
                    {
                        m_SubCanvases.Add((c, c.sortingOrder - rootBaseOrder));
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 显式执行静态绑定并从父容器创建窗口子 Scope（解决 A-P03）
        /// </summary>
        public void BuildScopeAndCreate(IObjectResolver parentResolver) => BuildScopeAndCreate(parentResolver, null);

        internal bool BuildScopeAndCreate(IObjectResolver parentResolver, ViewRoute route)
        {
            // 1. 显式调用静态组件绑定，生成 UIView
            ((IAutoBindHost)ViewHost).EnsureAutoBind(ViewHost.gameObject);

            // 2. 通过父容器纯代码创建窗口子容器作用域
            m_ChildScope = parentResolver.CreateScope(builder =>
            {
                builder.RegisterInstance<IWindowContext>(this);
                builder.RegisterInstance<IViewManager>(m_ViewManager);
                ViewHost.ConfigureWindow(builder);
            });

            // 3. 解析 Presenter 并完成关联与首次创建初始化
            var presenter = m_ChildScope.Resolve<IWindowPresenter>();
            if (route != null && !route.ValidatePresenter(presenter)) return false;
            Presenter = presenter;
            Presenter.InternalAttach(this, m_ChildScope);
            Presenter.InternalOnCreate();
            return true;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 设置根 Canvas 排序并按初始相对差值同步平移所有子 Canvas（解决 A-P08）
        /// </summary>
        public void SetSortingOrder(int rootSortingOrder)
        {
            if (m_RootCanvas != null)
            {
                m_RootCanvas.sortingOrder = rootSortingOrder;
            }

            for (int i = 0; i < m_SubCanvases.Count; i++)
            {
                var (subCanvas, offset) = m_SubCanvases[i];
                if (subCanvas != null)
                {
                    subCanvas.sortingOrder = rootSortingOrder + offset;
                }
            }
        }

        public void DeliverOpenArgs(IViewArgs args)
        {
            if (m_IsDisposed || IsClosing) return;
            HasOpenSession = true;
            Presenter?.InternalOnOpen(args ?? EmptyViewArgs.Instance);
        }

        public void SetActiveAndResume()
        {
            // 重算其他窗口的排序不能重播本窗口正在进行的入场。
            if (m_IsDisposed || IsClosing || State == WindowState.Destroyed || State == WindowState.OpenAndActive) return;
            int version = ++m_TransitionVersion;
            IsTransitioning = false;
            State = WindowState.OpenAndActive;
            List<Exception> errors = null;
            try { ViewHost.CancelTransition(); }
            catch (Exception error) { ViewLifecycleErrors.Add(ref errors, error); }
            if (version == m_TransitionVersion && !m_IsDisposed)
            {
                ViewHost.SetViewVisible(true);
                ViewHost.SetInteractable(false);
                try { Presenter?.InternalOnResume(); }
                catch (Exception error) { ViewLifecycleErrors.Add(ref errors, error); }
                if (version == m_TransitionVersion && State == WindowState.OpenAndActive && !m_IsDisposed)
                {
                    if (ViewHost.HasTransition)
                    {
                        IsTransitioning = true;
                        try { ViewHost.PlayEnterTransition(() => CompleteEnter(version)); }
                        catch (Exception error)
                        {
                            ViewLifecycleErrors.Add(ref errors, error);
                            CompleteEnter(version);
                        }
                    }
                    else ViewHost.SetInteractable(true);
                }
            }
            ViewLifecycleErrors.Throw(errors);
        }

        private void CompleteEnter(int version)
        {
            if (version != m_TransitionVersion || !IsTransitioning || m_IsDisposed || State != WindowState.OpenAndActive) return;
            IsTransitioning = false;
            if (ViewHost != null) ViewHost.SetInteractable(true);
        }

        public void Pause()
        {
            if (State == WindowState.OpenAndActive)
            {
                int version = ++m_TransitionVersion;
                IsTransitioning = false;
                State = WindowState.Paused;
                List<Exception> errors = null;
                try { ViewHost.CancelTransition(); }
                catch (Exception error) { ViewLifecycleErrors.Add(ref errors, error); }
                try { Presenter?.InternalOnPause(); }
                catch (Exception error) { ViewLifecycleErrors.Add(ref errors, error); }
                if (version == m_TransitionVersion && !m_IsDisposed && ViewHost != null)
                {
                    ViewHost.SetInteractable(false);
                    ViewHost.SetViewVisible(false);
                }
                ViewLifecycleErrors.Throw(errors);
            }
        }

        public void EnterClosedState(WindowState closedTargetState) => EnterClosedState(closedTargetState, null);

        public void EnterClosedState(WindowState closedTargetState, Action onExitTransitionComplete)
        {
            if (m_IsDisposed || IsClosing || State == WindowState.Destroyed ||
                (!HasOpenSession && (State == WindowState.Cached || State == WindowState.PendingDestroy))) return;
            int version = ++m_TransitionVersion;
            bool wasActive = State == WindowState.OpenAndActive;
            State = closedTargetState;
            HasOpenSession = false;
            IsTransitioning = false;
            IsClosing = true;
            List<Exception> errors = null;
            try
            {
                try { if (ViewHost != null) ViewHost.CancelTransition(); }
                catch (Exception error) { ViewLifecycleErrors.Add(ref errors, error); }
                if (ViewHost != null) ViewHost.SetInteractable(false);
                if (wasActive)
                {
                    try { Presenter?.InternalOnPause(); }
                    catch (Exception error) { ViewLifecycleErrors.Add(ref errors, error); }
                }
                try { Presenter?.InternalOnClose(); }
                catch (Exception error) { ViewLifecycleErrors.Add(ref errors, error); }
            }
            finally { IsClosing = false; }
            if (version == m_TransitionVersion && !m_IsDisposed)
            {
                IsTransitioning = true;
                try
                {
                    if (ViewHost != null && ViewHost.HasTransition && ViewHost.gameObject.activeInHierarchy)
                        ViewHost.PlayExitTransition(() => CompleteExit(version, onExitTransitionComplete));
                    else CompleteExit(version, onExitTransitionComplete);
                }
                catch (Exception error)
                {
                    ViewLifecycleErrors.Add(ref errors, error);
                    try { CompleteExit(version, onExitTransitionComplete); }
                    catch (Exception cleanupError) { ViewLifecycleErrors.Add(ref errors, cleanupError); }
                }
            }
            ViewLifecycleErrors.Throw(errors);
        }

        private void CompleteExit(int version, Action onCompleted)
        {
            if (version != m_TransitionVersion || !IsTransitioning || m_IsDisposed) return;
            IsTransitioning = false;
            try { if (ViewHost != null) ViewHost.SetViewVisible(false); }
            finally { onCompleted?.Invoke(); }
        }

        public void Tick(float deltaTime)
        {
            if (State == WindowState.OpenAndActive)
            {
                Presenter?.InternalTick(deltaTime);
            }
        }

        public void CloseSelf()
        {
            m_ViewManager.CloseView(InstanceId);
        }

        public Awaitable WaitClosedAsync(CancellationToken cancellationToken = default) =>
            m_ViewManager.WaitViewClosedAsync(InstanceId, cancellationToken);

        public void Dispose()
        {
            // DestroyImmediate 已先切换状态；状态不能代表 Presenter 与 Scope 已释放。
            if (m_IsDisposed) return;
            m_IsDisposed = true;
            ++m_TransitionVersion;
            IsTransitioning = false;
            bool wasActive = State == WindowState.OpenAndActive;
            bool wasOpen = HasOpenSession || wasActive || State == WindowState.Paused;
            HasOpenSession = false;
            State = WindowState.Destroyed;
            List<Exception> errors = null;
            try
            {
                try
                {
                    if (ViewHost != null)
                    {
                        ViewHost.CancelTransition();
                        ViewHost.SetInteractable(false);
                        ViewHost.SetViewVisible(false);
                    }
                }
                catch (Exception error) { ViewLifecycleErrors.Add(ref errors, error); }
                if (wasActive)
                {
                    try { Presenter?.InternalOnPause(); }
                    catch (Exception error) { ViewLifecycleErrors.Add(ref errors, error); }
                }
                if (wasOpen)
                {
                    try { Presenter?.InternalOnClose(); }
                    catch (Exception error) { ViewLifecycleErrors.Add(ref errors, error); }
                }
                try { Presenter?.InternalOnDestroy(); }
                catch (Exception error) { ViewLifecycleErrors.Add(ref errors, error); }
            }
            finally
            {
                Presenter = null;
                var scope = m_ChildScope;
                m_ChildScope = null;
                try { scope?.Dispose(); }
                catch (Exception error) { ViewLifecycleErrors.Add(ref errors, error); }
            }
            ViewLifecycleErrors.Throw(errors);
        }
    }

    internal static class ViewLifecycleErrors
    {
        internal static void Add(ref List<Exception> errors, Exception error)
        {
            errors ??= new List<Exception>();
            errors.Add(error);
        }

        internal static void Throw(List<Exception> errors)
        {
            if (errors == null) return;
            if (errors.Count == 1) ExceptionDispatchInfo.Capture(errors[0]).Throw();
            throw new AggregateException("窗口生命周期或资源释放失败。", errors);
        }
    }
}


==================== FILE: E:\Github Unity Projects\VContainer ComponentAutoBindTool\Assets\Third Party\ComponentAutoBindTool\Scripts\ViewCore\Presenters\WidgetPresenterBase.cs ====================

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Runtime.ExceptionServices;
using System.Threading;
using VContainer;

// ReSharper disable once CheckNamespace
namespace ComponentAutoBindTool.ViewCore
{
    public abstract class WidgetPresenterBase<TUIView> : IWidgetPresenter
        where TUIView : class
    {
        protected TUIView View { get; private set; }
        protected IWindowContext WindowContext { get; private set; }
        private IObjectResolver m_WindowScope;

        public bool IsVisible { get; private set; } = true;
        private bool m_IsOpenSessionActive;
        private bool m_IsResumed;
        private bool m_IsParentResumed;
        private bool m_IsPausing;
        private bool m_IsClosing;
        private bool m_IsDestroying;
        private bool m_IsDestroyed;
        private bool m_CloseRequested;
        private bool m_DestroyRequested;

        private readonly List<IDisposable> m_InstanceDisposables = new();
        private readonly List<IDisposable> m_OpenSessionDisposables = new();
        private CancellationTokenSource m_InstanceCts = new();
        private CancellationTokenSource m_OpenSessionCts;

        private readonly List<IWidgetPresenter> m_ChildWidgets = new();

        protected CancellationToken DestroyToken => 
            m_InstanceCts?.Token ?? CancellationToken.None;
        protected CancellationToken OpenSessionToken => 
            m_OpenSessionCts?.Token ?? CancellationToken.None;

        protected void AddToInstanceLifetime(IDisposable disposable)
        {
            if (disposable != null) m_InstanceDisposables.Add(disposable);
        }

        protected void AddToOpenSession(IDisposable disposable)
        {
            if (disposable != null) m_OpenSessionDisposables.Add(disposable);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 在当前 Widget 下继续绑定嵌套子 Widget（复用所属窗口 Scope，零额外容器开销）
        /// </summary>
        protected TChildPresenter BindWidget<TChildPresenter>(WidgetViewBase childWidgetHost)
            where TChildPresenter : class, IWidgetPresenter
        {
            if (m_IsClosing || m_IsDestroying || m_IsDestroyed)
            {
                throw new InvalidOperationException("正在关闭或已销毁的 Widget 不能再绑定子控件。");
            }
            if (childWidgetHost == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(childWidgetHost));

            ((IAutoBindHost)childWidgetHost).EnsureAutoBind(childWidgetHost.gameObject);
            object childUIView = childWidgetHost.GetGeneratedUIView();
            if (childUIView == null)
            {
                throw new InvalidOperationException($"[{childWidgetHost.name}] Widget 绑定失败，不能创建 Presenter。");
            }

            var childPresenter = m_WindowScope.Resolve<TChildPresenter>();
            childPresenter.InternalAttach(childUIView, WindowContext, m_WindowScope);

            m_ChildWidgets.Add(childPresenter);
            childPresenter.InternalOnCreate();

            if (m_IsOpenSessionActive)
            {
                childPresenter.InternalOnOpen();
                if (m_IsResumed)
                {
                    childPresenter.InternalOnResume();
                }
            }
            return childPresenter;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 控制子控件自身的局部显隐，并同步触发 OnResume / OnPause
        /// </summary>
        public void SetVisible(bool visible, WidgetViewBase hostComponent = null)
        {
            if (IsVisible == visible) return;
            IsVisible = visible;
            hostComponent?.SetWidgetVisible(visible);

            RefreshEffectiveState();
        }

        void IWidgetPresenter.InternalAttach(object uiView, IWindowContext windowContext, IObjectResolver windowScope)
        {
            View = (TUIView)uiView;
            WindowContext = windowContext;
            m_WindowScope = windowScope;
        }

        void IWidgetPresenter.InternalOnCreate() => OnCreate();

        void IWidgetPresenter.InternalOnOpen()
        {
            if (m_IsClosing || m_IsDestroying || m_IsDestroyed) return;
            ClearOpenSession();
            m_IsOpenSessionActive = true;
            m_OpenSessionCts = new CancellationTokenSource();

            OnOpen();

            for (int i = 0; i < m_ChildWidgets.Count; i++)
            {
                m_ChildWidgets[i].InternalOnOpen();
            }
        }

        void IWidgetPresenter.InternalOnResume()
        {
            if (m_IsClosing || m_IsDestroying || m_IsDestroyed) return;
            m_IsParentResumed = true;
            RefreshEffectiveState();
        }

        void IWidgetPresenter.InternalOnPause()
        {
            m_IsParentResumed = false;
            RefreshEffectiveState();
        }

        private void RefreshEffectiveState()
        {
            bool active = m_IsOpenSessionActive && m_IsParentResumed && IsVisible;
            if (m_IsResumed == active) return;
            if (active)
            {
                m_IsResumed = true;
                OnResume();
                for (int i = 0; i < m_ChildWidgets.Count && m_IsResumed; i++)
                {
                    m_ChildWidgets[i].InternalOnResume();
                }
            }
            else
            {
                List<Exception> exceptions = null;
                PauseRunningSubtree(ref exceptions);
                CompleteRequestedTransitions(ref exceptions);
                ThrowExceptions(exceptions);
            }
        }

        void IWidgetPresenter.InternalOnClose()
        {
            List<Exception> exceptions = null;
            CloseOpenSession(ref exceptions);
            CompleteRequestedTransitions(ref exceptions);
            ThrowExceptions(exceptions);
        }

        private void CloseOpenSession(ref List<Exception> exceptions)
        {
            if (!m_IsOpenSessionActive || m_IsClosing || m_IsDestroyed) return;
            if (m_IsPausing)
            {
                m_CloseRequested = true;
                return;
            }

            m_IsClosing = true;
            m_CloseRequested = false;
            // 先让会话失效，回调中再次 Close 不会进入同一轮关闭。
            m_IsOpenSessionActive = false;
            m_IsParentResumed = false;
            try
            {
                PauseRunningSubtree(ref exceptions);
                for (int i = m_ChildWidgets.Count - 1; i >= 0; i--)
                {
                    try
                    {
                        m_ChildWidgets[i].InternalOnClose();
                    }
                    catch (Exception exception)
                    {
                        AddException(ref exceptions, exception);
                    }
                }
                try
                {
                    OnClose();
                }
                catch (Exception exception)
                {
                    AddException(ref exceptions, exception);
                }
            }
            finally
            {
                ClearOpenSession(ref exceptions);
                m_IsClosing = false;
            }
        }

        void IWidgetPresenter.InternalOnDestroy()
        {
            List<Exception> exceptions = null;
            Destroy(ref exceptions);
            CompleteRequestedTransitions(ref exceptions);
            ThrowExceptions(exceptions);
        }

        private void Destroy(ref List<Exception> exceptions)
        {
            if (m_IsDestroyed || m_IsDestroying) return;
            if (m_IsClosing || m_IsPausing)
            {
                // 让当前子树的 Pause/Close 完成后再进入销毁阶段。
                m_DestroyRequested = true;
                return;
            }

            m_IsDestroying = true;
            m_DestroyRequested = false;
            try
            {
                CloseOpenSession(ref exceptions);
                for (int i = m_ChildWidgets.Count - 1; i >= 0; i--)
                {
                    try
                    {
                        m_ChildWidgets[i].InternalOnDestroy();
                    }
                    catch (Exception exception)
                    {
                        AddException(ref exceptions, exception);
                    }
                }
                try
                {
                    OnDestroy();
                }
                catch (Exception exception)
                {
                    AddException(ref exceptions, exception);
                }
            }
            finally
            {
                m_ChildWidgets.Clear();
                m_IsOpenSessionActive = false;
                m_IsResumed = false;
                m_IsParentResumed = false;
                ClearOpenSession(ref exceptions);
                CancelAndDispose(ref m_InstanceCts, ref exceptions);
                ReleaseDisposables(m_InstanceDisposables, ref exceptions);
                m_CloseRequested = false;
                m_DestroyRequested = false;
                m_IsDestroyed = true;
                m_IsDestroying = false;
            }
        }

        private void PauseRunningSubtree(ref List<Exception> exceptions)
        {
            if (!m_IsResumed) return;

            m_IsResumed = false;
            m_IsPausing = true;
            try
            {
                for (int i = 0; i < m_ChildWidgets.Count; i++)
                {
                    try
                    {
                        m_ChildWidgets[i].InternalOnPause();
                    }
                    catch (Exception exception)
                    {
                        AddException(ref exceptions, exception);
                    }
                }
                try
                {
                    OnPause();
                }
                catch (Exception exception)
                {
                    AddException(ref exceptions, exception);
                }
            }
            finally
            {
                m_IsPausing = false;
            }
        }

        private void CompleteRequestedTransitions(ref List<Exception> exceptions)
        {
            if (m_IsPausing || m_IsClosing || m_IsDestroying || m_IsDestroyed) return;
            if (m_DestroyRequested)
            {
                Destroy(ref exceptions);
            }
            else if (m_CloseRequested)
            {
                CloseOpenSession(ref exceptions);
                if (m_DestroyRequested) Destroy(ref exceptions);
            }
        }

        void IWidgetPresenter.InternalTick(float deltaTime)
        {
            if (!IsVisible || !m_IsOpenSessionActive || !m_IsResumed) return;

            OnWidgetTick(deltaTime);
            for (int i = 0; i < m_ChildWidgets.Count; i++)
            {
                m_ChildWidgets[i].InternalTick(deltaTime);
            }
        }

        private void ClearOpenSession()
        {
            List<Exception> exceptions = null;
            ClearOpenSession(ref exceptions);
            ThrowExceptions(exceptions);
        }

        private void ClearOpenSession(ref List<Exception> exceptions)
        {
            CancelAndDispose(ref m_OpenSessionCts, ref exceptions);
            ReleaseDisposables(m_OpenSessionDisposables, ref exceptions);
        }

        private static void CancelAndDispose(ref CancellationTokenSource source, ref List<Exception> exceptions)
        {
            CancellationTokenSource ownedSource = source;
            // 取消回调可以重入生命周期入口，先移走资源所有权。
            source = null;
            if (ownedSource == null) return;
            try
            {
                ownedSource.Cancel();
            }
            catch (Exception exception)
            {
                AddException(ref exceptions, exception);
            }
            finally
            {
                ownedSource.Dispose();
            }
        }

        private static void ReleaseDisposables(List<IDisposable> disposables, ref List<Exception> exceptions)
        {
            while (disposables.Count > 0)
            {
                int index = disposables.Count - 1;
                IDisposable disposable = disposables[index];
                // 调用用户清理动作前移除，避免重入时重复释放。
                disposables.RemoveAt(index);
                try
                {
                    disposable?.Dispose();
                }
                catch (Exception exception)
                {
                    AddException(ref exceptions, exception);
                }
            }
        }

        private static void AddException(ref List<Exception> exceptions, Exception exception)
        {
            exceptions ??= new List<Exception>();
            exceptions.Add(exception);
        }

        private static void ThrowExceptions(List<Exception> exceptions)
        {
            if (exceptions == null) return;
            if (exceptions.Count == 1)
            {
                ExceptionDispatchInfo.Capture(exceptions[0]).Throw();
            }
            throw new AggregateException("Widget 生命周期回调或资源释放失败。", exceptions);
        }

        protected virtual void OnCreate() { }
        protected virtual void OnOpen() { }
        protected virtual void OnResume() { }
        protected virtual void OnPause() { }
        protected virtual void OnClose() { }
        protected virtual void OnDestroy() { }
        protected virtual void OnWidgetTick(float deltaTime) { }
    }
}


==================== FILE: E:\Github Unity Projects\VContainer ComponentAutoBindTool\Assets\Third Party\ComponentAutoBindTool\Scripts\ViewCore\Presenters\WindowPresenterBase.cs ====================

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Runtime.ExceptionServices;
using System.Threading;
using VContainer;

namespace ComponentAutoBindTool.ViewCore
{
    /// <summary>
    /// 窗口 Presenter 泛型基类（支持强类型开窗参数 TArgs）
    /// </summary>
    public abstract class WindowPresenterBase<TUIView, TArgs> : IWindowPresenter, IWindowArgsContract
        where TUIView : class
        where TArgs : class, IViewArgs
    {
        protected TUIView View { get; }
        public Type ArgsType => typeof(TArgs);
        protected IWindowContext Context { get; private set; }
        private IObjectResolver m_WindowScope;

        private bool m_IsOpenSessionActive;
        private bool m_IsResumed;
        private bool m_IsPausing;
        private bool m_IsClosing;
        private bool m_IsDestroying;
        private bool m_IsDestroyed;
        private bool m_CloseRequested;
        private bool m_DestroyRequested;

        // 1. 实例存活期清理袋（OnCreate -> OnDestroy，在 NonDestroy 缓存期间保留）
        private readonly List<IDisposable> m_InstanceDisposables = new();
        private CancellationTokenSource m_InstanceCts = new();

        // 2. 打开活动期清理袋（OnOpen -> OnClose，每次窗口关闭进缓存或重复刷新时强制自动清空）
        private readonly List<IDisposable> m_OpenSessionDisposables = new();
        private CancellationTokenSource m_OpenSessionCts;

        // 3. 归属于当前窗口的逻辑子控件树
        private readonly List<IWidgetPresenter> m_ChildWidgets = new();

        protected CancellationToken DestroyToken => m_InstanceCts?.Token ?? CancellationToken.None;
        protected CancellationToken OpenSessionToken => m_OpenSessionCts?.Token ?? CancellationToken.None;

        protected WindowPresenterBase(TUIView view)
        {
            View = view;
        }

        protected void AddToInstanceLifetime(IDisposable disposable)
        {
            if (disposable != null) m_InstanceDisposables.Add(disposable);
        }

        protected void AddToOpenSession(IDisposable disposable)
        {
            if (disposable != null) m_OpenSessionDisposables.Add(disposable);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 绑定窗口内的子控件 Widget（共享当前窗口子容器 Scope，自动纳入窗口生命周期级联树）
        /// </summary>
        protected TWidgetPresenter BindWidget<TWidgetPresenter>(WidgetViewBase widgetHost)
            where TWidgetPresenter : class, IWidgetPresenter
        {
            if (m_IsClosing || m_IsDestroying || m_IsDestroyed)
            {
                throw new InvalidOperationException("正在关闭或已销毁的窗口不能再绑定子控件。");
            }
            if (widgetHost == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(widgetHost));

            // 1. 显式触发子控件的静态绑定
            ((IAutoBindHost)widgetHost).EnsureAutoBind(widgetHost.gameObject);
            object widgetUIView = widgetHost.GetGeneratedUIView();
            if (widgetUIView == null)
            {
                throw new InvalidOperationException($"[{widgetHost.name}] Widget 绑定失败，不能创建 Presenter。");
            }

            // 2. 从当前窗口子容器解析 WidgetPresenter（自动注入全局/窗口服务）
            var widgetPresenter = m_WindowScope.Resolve<TWidgetPresenter>();
            widgetPresenter.InternalAttach(widgetUIView, Context, m_WindowScope);

            // 3. 加入逻辑树并同步当前窗口所处生命周期阶段
            m_ChildWidgets.Add(widgetPresenter);
            widgetPresenter.InternalOnCreate();

            if (m_IsOpenSessionActive)
            {
                widgetPresenter.InternalOnOpen();
                if (m_IsResumed)
                {
                    widgetPresenter.InternalOnResume();
                }
            }

            return widgetPresenter;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 动态解绑并销毁指定的子控件 Widget
        /// </summary>
        protected void UnbindWidget(IWidgetPresenter widgetPresenter)
        {
            if (widgetPresenter != null && m_ChildWidgets.Remove(widgetPresenter))
            {
                widgetPresenter.InternalOnDestroy();
            }
        }

        void IWindowPresenter.InternalAttach(IWindowContext context, IObjectResolver windowScope)
        {
            Context = context;
            m_WindowScope = windowScope;
        }

        void IWindowPresenter.InternalOnCreate() => OnCreate();

        void IWindowPresenter.InternalOnOpen(IViewArgs args)
        {
            if (m_IsClosing || m_IsDestroying || m_IsDestroyed) return;
            // 若在已打开态重复调用 ShowView 刷新数据，先重置上一轮 OpenSession 订阅
            ClearOpenSession();
            if (m_IsDestroyed) return;
            m_IsOpenSessionActive = true;
            m_OpenSessionCts = new CancellationTokenSource();

            OnOpen(args as TArgs);

            for (int i = 0; i < m_ChildWidgets.Count && m_IsOpenSessionActive;)
            {
                IWidgetPresenter child = m_ChildWidgets[i];
                child.InternalOnOpen();
                if (i < m_ChildWidgets.Count && ReferenceEquals(child, m_ChildWidgets[i])) i++;
            }
        }

        void IWindowPresenter.InternalOnResume()
        {
            if (!m_IsOpenSessionActive || m_IsClosing || m_IsDestroying || m_IsDestroyed || m_IsResumed) return;
            m_IsResumed = true;
            OnResume();

            for (int i = 0; i < m_ChildWidgets.Count && m_IsResumed;)
            {
                IWidgetPresenter child = m_ChildWidgets[i];
                child.InternalOnResume();
                if (i < m_ChildWidgets.Count && ReferenceEquals(child, m_ChildWidgets[i])) i++;
            }
        }

        void IWindowPresenter.InternalOnPause()
        {
            List<Exception> exceptions = null;
            PauseRunningSubtree(ref exceptions);
            CompleteRequestedTransitions(ref exceptions);
            ThrowExceptions(exceptions);
        }

        void IWindowPresenter.InternalOnClose()
        {
            List<Exception> exceptions = null;
            CloseOpenSession(ref exceptions);
            CompleteRequestedTransitions(ref exceptions);
            ThrowExceptions(exceptions);
        }

        private void CloseOpenSession(ref List<Exception> exceptions)
        {
            if (!m_IsOpenSessionActive || m_IsClosing || m_IsDestroyed) return;
            if (m_IsPausing)
            {
                m_CloseRequested = true;
                return;
            }

            m_IsClosing = true;
            m_CloseRequested = false;
            // 先让会话失效，回调中的 Close 不会重复进入当前关闭阶段。
            m_IsOpenSessionActive = false;
            try
            {
                PauseRunningSubtree(ref exceptions);
                for (int i = m_ChildWidgets.Count - 1; i >= 0;)
                {
                    IWidgetPresenter child = m_ChildWidgets[i];
                    try
                    {
                        child.InternalOnClose();
                    }
                    catch (Exception exception)
                    {
                        AddException(ref exceptions, exception);
                    }
                    if (i < m_ChildWidgets.Count && ReferenceEquals(child, m_ChildWidgets[i]))
                    {
                        i--;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        int movedIndex = m_ChildWidgets.IndexOf(child);
                        i = movedIndex >= 0 ? movedIndex - 1 : Math.Min(i - 1, m_ChildWidgets.Count - 1);
                    }
                }
                try
                {
                    OnClose();
                }
                catch (Exception exception)
                {
                    AddException(ref exceptions, exception);
                }
            }
            finally
            {
                ClearOpenSession(ref exceptions);
                m_IsClosing = false;
            }
        }

        void IWindowPresenter.InternalOnDestroy()
        {
            List<Exception> exceptions = null;
            Destroy(ref exceptions);
            CompleteRequestedTransitions(ref exceptions);
            ThrowExceptions(exceptions);
        }

        private void Destroy(ref List<Exception> exceptions)
        {
            if (m_IsDestroyed || m_IsDestroying) return;
            if (m_IsClosing || m_IsPausing)
            {
                // 等当前子树 Pause/Close 完成后再销毁，避免阶段交错。
                m_DestroyRequested = true;
                return;
            }

            m_IsDestroying = true;
            m_DestroyRequested = false;
            try
            {
                CloseOpenSession(ref exceptions);
                while (m_ChildWidgets.Count > 0)
                {
                    int index = m_ChildWidgets.Count - 1;
                    IWidgetPresenter child = m_ChildWidgets[index];
                    m_ChildWidgets.RemoveAt(index);
                    try
                    {
                        child.InternalOnDestroy();
                    }
                    catch (Exception exception)
                    {
                        AddException(ref exceptions, exception);
                    }
                }
                try
                {
                    OnDestroy();
                }
                catch (Exception exception)
                {
                    AddException(ref exceptions, exception);
                }
            }
            finally
            {
                m_IsOpenSessionActive = false;
                m_IsResumed = false;
                ClearOpenSession(ref exceptions);
                CancelAndDispose(ref m_InstanceCts, ref exceptions);
                ReleaseDisposables(m_InstanceDisposables, ref exceptions);
                m_CloseRequested = false;
                m_DestroyRequested = false;
                m_IsDestroyed = true;
                m_IsDestroying = false;
            }
        }

        private void PauseRunningSubtree(ref List<Exception> exceptions)
        {
            if (!m_IsResumed) return;

            m_IsResumed = false;
            m_IsPausing = true;
            try
            {
                for (int i = 0; i < m_ChildWidgets.Count;)
                {
                    IWidgetPresenter child = m_ChildWidgets[i];
                    try
                    {
                        child.InternalOnPause();
                    }
                    catch (Exception exception)
                    {
                        AddException(ref exceptions, exception);
                    }
                    // 子控件可在回调中解绑自身，索引不能跳过移入当前位置的兄弟。
                    if (i < m_ChildWidgets.Count && ReferenceEquals(child, m_ChildWidgets[i])) i++;
                }
                try
                {
                    OnPause();
                }
                catch (Exception exception)
                {
                    AddException(ref exceptions, exception);
                }
            }
            finally
            {
                m_IsPausing = false;
            }
        }

        private void CompleteRequestedTransitions(ref List<Exception> exceptions)
        {
            if (m_IsPausing || m_IsClosing || m_IsDestroying || m_IsDestroyed) return;
            if (m_DestroyRequested)
            {
                Destroy(ref exceptions);
            }
            else if (m_CloseRequested)
            {
                CloseOpenSession(ref exceptions);
                if (m_DestroyRequested) Destroy(ref exceptions);
            }
        }

        void IWindowPresenter.InternalTick(float deltaTime)
        {
            if (!m_IsOpenSessionActive || !m_IsResumed) return;

            OnWindowTick(deltaTime);
            for (int i = 0; i < m_ChildWidgets.Count; i++)
            {
                m_ChildWidgets[i].InternalTick(deltaTime);
            }
        }

        private void ClearOpenSession()
        {
            List<Exception> exceptions = null;
            ClearOpenSession(ref exceptions);
            ThrowExceptions(exceptions);
        }

        private void ClearOpenSession(ref List<Exception> exceptions)
        {
            CancelAndDispose(ref m_OpenSessionCts, ref exceptions);
            ReleaseDisposables(m_OpenSessionDisposables, ref exceptions);
        }

        private static void CancelAndDispose(ref CancellationTokenSource source, ref List<Exception> exceptions)
        {
            CancellationTokenSource ownedSource = source;
            // 取消回调可能重入，先转移所有权再调用用户代码。
            source = null;
            if (ownedSource == null) return;
            try
            {
                ownedSource.Cancel();
            }
            catch (Exception exception)
            {
                AddException(ref exceptions, exception);
            }
            finally
            {
                ownedSource.Dispose();
            }
        }

        private static void ReleaseDisposables(List<IDisposable> disposables, ref List<Exception> exceptions)
        {
            while (disposables.Count > 0)
            {
                int index = disposables.Count - 1;
                IDisposable disposable = disposables[index];
                disposables.RemoveAt(index);
                try
                {
                    disposable?.Dispose();
                }
                catch (Exception exception)
                {
                    AddException(ref exceptions, exception);
                }
            }
        }

        private static void AddException(ref List<Exception> exceptions, Exception exception)
        {
            exceptions ??= new List<Exception>();
            exceptions.Add(exception);
        }

        private static void ThrowExceptions(List<Exception> exceptions)
        {
            if (exceptions == null) return;
            if (exceptions.Count == 1)
            {
                ExceptionDispatchInfo.Capture(exceptions[0]).Throw();
            }
            throw new AggregateException("窗口生命周期回调或资源释放失败。", exceptions);
        }

        protected virtual void OnCreate() { }
        protected abstract void OnOpen(TArgs args);
        protected virtual void OnResume() { }
        protected virtual void OnPause() { }
        protected virtual void OnClose() { }
        protected virtual void OnDestroy() { }
        protected virtual void OnWindowTick(float deltaTime) { }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 无需特定强类型参数（或使用通用 IViewArgs / LegacyViewArgs）的便捷窗口 Presenter 基类
    /// </summary>
    public abstract class WindowPresenterBase<TUIView> : WindowPresenterBase<TUIView, IViewArgs>
        where TUIView : class
    {
        protected WindowPresenterBase(TUIView view) : base(view) { }
    }
}


==================== FILE: E:\Github Unity Projects\VContainer ComponentAutoBindTool\Assets\Third Party\ComponentAutoBindTool\Scripts\ViewCore\ViewHosts\ViewBase.cs ====================

using System;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;
using VContainer;

// ReSharper disable once CheckNamespace
namespace ComponentAutoBindTool.ViewCore
{
    [RequireComponent(typeof(Canvas),
        typeof(GraphicRaycaster),
        typeof(Core.ComponentAutoBindTool))]
    public abstract class ViewBase : MonoBehaviour, IAutoBindHost
    {
        [Header("窗口调度与层级配置")]
        public UILayer Layer = UILayer.Normal;
        public WindowInstanceMode InstanceMode = WindowInstanceMode.Singleton;
        public ViewCoverMode CoverMode = ViewCoverMode.PausePrevious;
        public ViewCloseStrategy CloseStrategy = ViewCloseStrategy.NonDestroy;

        [Tooltip("当 CloseStrategy 为 DestroyDelayed 时的延迟销毁秒数（使用真实时间）")]
        public float DestroyDelaySeconds = 3f;

        [Tooltip("是否允许被返回键 (TryNavigateBack) 关闭")]
        public bool AllowNavigateBack = true;

        private GraphicRaycaster m_GraphicRaycaster;
        private IViewTransition m_Transition;
        private bool m_TransitionSearched;

        /// <summary>
        /// 当前宿主是否挂载了自定义入场/退场过渡动画组件
        /// </summary>
        public bool HasTransition
        {
            get
            {
                EnsureTransitionResolved();
                return m_Transition != null;
            }
        }

        // 由生成代码重写；默认实现允许宿主在首次生成前编译。
        public virtual void EnsureAutoBind(GameObject go)
        {
            ViewLogger.Error($"[{name}] 尚未生成 AutoBind 绑定代码。");
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 由子类实现，向当前窗口的 VContainer 子容器注册 UIView 与 Presenter
        /// </summary>
        public abstract void ConfigureWindow(IContainerBuilder builder);

        /// <summary>
        /// 标准窗口注册辅助方法：注册生成的 UIView 强类型实例与窗口 Presenter
        /// </summary>
        protected void RegisterWindow<TPresenter, TUIView>(IContainerBuilder builder, TUIView uiView)
            where TPresenter : class, IWindowPresenter
            where TUIView : class
        {
            if (uiView == null)
            {
                throw new InvalidOperationException($"[{name}] RegisterWindow 失败：UIView 为 null，请先生成并检查 AutoBind 绑定。");
            }

            builder.RegisterInstance(uiView).AsSelf();
            builder.Register<TPresenter>(Lifetime.Scoped)
                   .As<IWindowPresenter>()
                   .AsSelf();
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 控制窗口根 GraphicRaycaster 交互开关（用于过渡动画期间防连点）
        /// </summary>
        public virtual void SetInteractable(bool interactable)
        {
            if (m_GraphicRaycaster == null && this != null)
            {
                TryGetComponent(out m_GraphicRaycaster);
            }

            if (m_GraphicRaycaster != null && m_GraphicRaycaster.enabled != interactable)
            {
                m_GraphicRaycaster.enabled = interactable;
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 表现层显示钩子
        /// </summary>
        public virtual void SetViewVisible(bool visible)
        {
            if (this != null && gameObject != null && gameObject.activeSelf != visible)
            {
                gameObject.SetActive(visible);
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 播放入场过渡（未挂载 IViewTransition 时同步立即完成，零延迟）
        /// </summary>
        public virtual void PlayEnterTransition(Action onCompleted)
        {
            EnsureTransitionResolved();
            if (m_Transition == null)
            {
                onCompleted?.Invoke();
                return;
            }

            m_Transition.PlayEnter(this, onCompleted);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 播放退场过渡（未挂载 IViewTransition 时同步立即完成，零延迟）
        /// </summary>
        public virtual void PlayExitTransition(Action onCompleted)
        {
            EnsureTransitionResolved();
            if (m_Transition == null)
            {
                onCompleted?.Invoke();
                return;
            }

            m_Transition.PlayExit(this, onCompleted);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 中断正在进行的过渡动画
        /// </summary>
        public virtual void CancelTransition()
        {
            EnsureTransitionResolved();
            m_Transition?.Cancel(this);
        }

        private void EnsureTransitionResolved()
        {
            if (m_TransitionSearched || this == null) return;
            m_TransitionSearched = true;
            TryGetComponent(out m_Transition);
        }
    }
}

using UnityEngine;

// ReSharper disable once CheckNamespace
namespace ComponentAutoBindTool.ViewCore
{
    /// <summary>
    /// 局部子控件 (UIWidget) MonoBehaviour 宿主基类
    /// </summary>
    [RequireComponent(typeof(Core.ComponentAutoBindTool))]
    public abstract class WidgetViewBase : MonoBehaviour, IAutoBindHost
    {
        // 默认实现允许首次生成前编译和挂载宿主。
        public virtual void EnsureAutoBind(GameObject go)
        {
            ViewLogger.Error($"[{name}] 尚未生成 Widget AutoBind 绑定代码。");
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 返回生成代码中的 view 字段（待后续微调生成器后可自动生成此方法）
        /// </summary>
        public virtual object GetGeneratedUIView()
        {
            ViewLogger.Error($"[{name}] 尚未生成 Widget UIView，请执行生成代码。");
            return null;
        }

        public virtual void SetWidgetVisible(bool visible)
        {
            if (gameObject.activeSelf != visible)
            {
                gameObject.SetActive(visible);
            }
        }
    }
}
